naṉṉūl mūlamum caṅkara namaccivāyaruraiyum

    • ((nāṉkāvatu iṭaiccolliyal))

((nūṟ ciṟappuppāyiram))

NA_ca_0

malartalai yulakiṉ malkiru ḷakala
ilakoḷi parappi yāvaiyum viḷakkum
parutiyi ṉorutā ṉāki mutalī
ṟoppaḷa vācai muṉivikan tuyarnta
5 aṟputa mūrttitaṉ ṉalartaru taṉmaiyiṉ
maṉaviru ḷiriya māṇporuṇ muḻuvatum
muṉivaṟa varuḷiya mūvaṟu moḻiyuḷum
kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭam
eṉunāṉ kellaiyi ṉiruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ
10 arumporu ḷaintaiyu miyāvaru muṇarat
tokaivakai viriyiṟ ṟarukeṉat tuṉṉār
ikalaṟa nūṟi yirunila muḻuvatum
taṉateṉak kōlit taṉmata vāraṇam
ticaitoṟu niṟuviya tiṟaluṟu tolcīrk
15 karuṅkaḻal veṇkuṭaik kārnikar vaṇkait
tiruntiya ceṅkōṟ cīya kaṅkaṉ
aruṅkalai vinōta ṉamarā paraṇaṉ
moḻintaṉa ṉāka muṉṉōr nūliṉ
vaḻiyē naṉṉūṟ peyariṉ vakuttaṉaṉ
20 poṉmatiṟ caṉakaic caṉmati muṉiyaruḷ
paṉṉaruñ ciṟappiṟ pavaṇanti
eṉṉu nāmat tiruntavat tōṉē.

eṉpatu pāyiram.
malartalai yulakiṟ palanū lāyntu
ceyvatun tavirvatum peṟuvatu muṟuvatum
uyvatu maṟiyē ṉoruporu ḷāka
naṉṉeṟi piṟaḻā naṟṟavat tōrpeṟum
5 taṉṉaṭit tāmarai tanteṉai yāṇṭa
tiruvā vaṭutuṟai tēcika ṉākiya
karuṇaiyaṅ kaṭalaiyeṉ kaṇṇaiviṭṭakalāc
cuvāmi nāta kuravaṉai yaṉutiṉam
maṉamoḻi meykaḷil vaṇaṅkiyava ṉaruḷāṟ
10 poṉmalai yeṉavip puvipukaḻ perumai
maṉṉiya vūṟṟu malaimaru tappaṉ
muttamiḻp pulamaiyu muṟaiyara curimaiyum
ittalat teytiya viṟaimaka ṉātaliṉ
naṉṉūṟ kurainī navaiyaṟac ceytu
15 paṉṉūṟ pulavarmuṉ pakartiyeṉ ṟiyampaliṉ
naṉṉā valarmuka nakainā ṇāmē
eṉṉā liyaṉṟavai yiyaṟṟumin nūluḷ,

ippāyiram eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, ciṟappup pāyirat tilakkaṇamāka mēl vakukkappaṭum ākkiyōṉ peyar mutaliyaṉa uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(itaṉ poruḷ), malartalai yulakiṉ paranta iṭattaiyuṭaiya ulakattiṉkaṇṇē, malku iruḷ akala - niṟainta kaṇṇiruḷ keṭa, ilaku oḷi parappi - viḷaṅkum katirai virittu, yāvaiyum viḷakkum - kaṭpoṟikku viṭayamākiya uruvamaṉaittiṉaiyum kāṭṭum, parutiyiṉ - cūriyaṉaippōla, orutāṉāki - ulakuk kellām tāṉ oru mutalēyāki, mutal īṟu oppu aḷavu ācai muṉivu ikantu uyarnta - tōṟṟamum oṭukkamum uvamaiyum aḷavum viruppum veṟuppumākiya avaṟṟai iyalpākavē nīṅki niṟṟalāl talaivaṉākiya aṟputa mūrtti - ñāṉamē tirumēṉiyākavuṭaiyāṉ, taṉ alartaru taṉmaiyiṉ - taṉatu virinta taṉmaiyākiya karuṇaiyiṉālē, maṉaviruḷ iriya - uyirkaḷiṉ maṉattiruḷākiya avittai keṭa, māṇporuḷ muḻuvatum - māṭcimaippaṭṭa aṟamporuḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṉum nāṉku poruḷaiyum, muṉivu aṟa aruḷiya - viruppuṭaṉ aruḷicceyta, mūvaṟu moḻiyuḷum - patiṉeṇṉilattu moḻikaḷuḷḷum, kuṇakaṭal kumari kuṭakam vēṅkaṭameṉum nāṉku ellaiyiṉ iruntamiḻk kaṭaluḷ - innāṉkellaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nilattu moḻiyākiya periya tamiḻeṉṉuṅkaṭaluḷ, arum poruḷaintaiyum - avvaṟa mutaṟ poruḷ nāṉkaiyumuṇartaṟkuk karuviyāy arumaiyavākiya eḻuttuc coṟporuḷ yāppu aṇiyeṉṉum aintu poruḷaiyum, yāvarumuṇara - puttiyāṉ valiyōrēyaṉṟi eḷiyōru muṇara, tokai vakai viriyiṉ tarukeṉa - immuttiṟamum oruṅkamaittuk kūṟappaṭum yāppiṉāṟ pāṭit tarukaveṉa, tuṉṉār ikal aṟa nūṟi - pakaivaratu pakaimaikeṭa avarait tuṇittu, irunilamuḻuvatum taṉateṉak kōli - periya pūmiyaṉaittiṉaiyum taṉṉu#aiṭuyatākap paṟṟikkoṇṭu, taṉmatavāraṇam ticaitoṟum niṟuviya tiṟaluṟu tolcīr - taṉ matayāṉaikaḷai eṭṭuttikkilum ticaikkaḷiṟukaḷ pōla niṟuttiya veṟṟi mikunta taṉṉaḷavēyaṉṟit taṉtātai mūtātai mutaliyōrait toṭṭu varuṅ kīrttiyiṉaiyum, karuṅkaḻal - perumai poruntiya vīrakkaḻalaiyum, veṇkuṭai - veṇkoṟṟak kuṭaiyiṉaiyum, kārnikar vaṇkai mēkattaippōlak kaimmāṟu karutātu koṭukkum vaṇkaiyiṉaiyum, tiruntiya ceṅkōl - kōṭāta ceṅkōliṉaiyumuṭaiya, cīyakaṅkaṉ - ciṅkam pōṉṟa kaṅkaṉ, aruṅkalai vinōtaṉ - ariyanūlkaḷai ārāytalē poḻutu pōkkum viḷaiyāṭṭākavuṭaiyāṉ, amarāparaṇaṉ - taṉṉakattu viḻuppuṇpaṭa amar ceytalaiyē āparaṇamākavuṭaiyāṉ, moḻintaṉaṉāka - coṉṉāṉāka, muṉṉōr nūliṉ vaḻiyē - tollāciriyar nūliṉvaḻiyē naṉṉūṟ peyariṉ vakuttaṉaṉ - naṉṉūleṉṉum peyariṉāṟ ceytaṉaṉ, poṉ matil caṉakai - poṉmatil cūḻnta caṉakāpurattuḷ irukkum, caṉmatimuṉiyaruḷ - caṉmati muṉivaṉ peṟṟa, paṉṉaruñciṟappiṟ pavaṇanti eṉṉum nāmattu - collutaṟkariya ñāṉavoḻukkac ciṟappiṉaiyum pavaṇantiyeṉṉum peyariṉaiyum uṭaiya, iruntavattōṉ - periya tavattiṉaiyuṭaiyōṉeṉṟavāṟu.

itaṉuḷ pavaṇantiyeṉa ākkiyōṉ peyarum, muṉṉōr nūliṉvaḻiyēyeṉa vaḻiyum, nāṉkellaiyeṉa ellaiyum, naṉṉūleṉa nūṟpeyarum, tokaivakaiviriyeṉa yāppum, arumporuḷainteṉa nutaliyaporuḷum, cīyakaṅkaṉ tarukeṉa moḻintaṉaṉeṉak kēṭpōrum, māṇporuṇmuḻuvatumeṉap payaṉum, cīyakaṅkaṉeṉavē avaṉ kālattuppāṭi avaṉ avaikkaḷattu ēṟṟiyateṉak kālamum kaḷaṉum, yāvarumuṇara veṉak kāraṇamum uṇarttiyavāṟu kāṇka.

tarukaveṉak kēṭpōraik kēṭpōreṉṉātu innūlai araṅkēṟṟuḻik kēṭpōraik kēṭpōreṉpārumuḷar; kaḷaṉeṉṟatu kēṭpōravaiyaiyātaliṉ atu kūṟiyatu kūṟalāmeṉka. nūlaik kūṟuvōrkkum kēṭpōrkkum payaṉ aṟam mutaliya nāṉkumeṉpatu tōṉṟa "nāṟporuṭpayattōṭu" (cū. 4) eṉṟum, "aṟamporu ḷiṉpamvī ṭaṭaitaṉūṟ payaṉē" (cū. 10) eṉṟum kūṟuvataṟintum yāvaru muṇartalaip payaṉeṉavum kūṟupa. aṉṟiyum, yāvaru muṇara vēṇṭuvār. vēṭṭaṉavum avvaṟamutaliya payaṉāṉmēyātalāṉum atu poruntāteṉka.

nālvakaiyāppiṉuḷ tokaiviriyāppeṉa oṉṟu pōntataṉṟit tokaivakaiviriyeṉap pōntatillaiyālōveṉiṉ, naṭuniṉṟa vakai piṉṉiṉṟa viriyainōkkiṟ ṟokaiyākavum, muṉṉiṉṟa tokaiyainōkkiṉ viriyākavu maṭaṅkutaliṉ itu tokaiviri yāppeṉṟataṉpāṟ paṭumeṉka. eṉavē tokai viriyeṉa iraṇṭāyvariṉum marattiṉatu parāraiyiṉiṉṟum kavaṭu kōṭu kompu vaḷār palavāy oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭar paṭṭeḻuntu niṟṟalpōl tokaiyiṉiṉṟum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpaṭap pakukkappaṭṭup palavāy variṉum tokaiviriyāppōyāmeṉka.

nutaliyaporuḷ arumporuḷainteṉa uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ, innūluṭ kūṟiya poruḷ yāppu aṇiyeṉṉum mūṉṟatikāraṅkaḷum akkālattuḷḷaṉa pōlum. ippāyiram oru nūṟkurittātaliṟ ciṟappuppāyiramāmeṉka.

((uraicciṟappuppāyiram))

((viruttam))

(1) naṉṉūluṭ karuttulakō raṟiyavurai ceykaveṉa narēntra ciṅkam
teṉṉūṟṟu malaimarutap paṉpukalap poruḷviḷaṅkac ceytāṉ pāril
ennūṟku meḻuttoṭucoṟ poruḷaṟicaṅ karanamacci vāya ṉeṉṉum
paṉṉūṟcen tamiḻppulavaṉ caivacikā maṇinellaip patiyi ṉāṉē.

(2) akattiyantol kāppiyamē mutaliyamuṉ ṉūlkaḷpala vāyntu muppāl
pakutta tiru vaḷḷuvarē mutaṉñāṉa nūlkaḷelām pakalcey vāṉpōl
ikattilurai ceyavalacaṅ karanamacci vāyaṉeṉum iyaṟpēr peṟṟa
takaippulava ṉaṉṉūluk kuraivakutta toruviyappō tamiḻval lōrē.

(3) muṉṉūṟku malayamuṉi taṉṉūṟkum puviyiṭattu mutiyō rāṟcol
ennūṟkun tamiḻkkaṭalu ḷiṉiyiyaṟṟu nūṟkumvakai itaṉāṟ kāṭṭi
naṉṉūṟku viritturaicey tāṉṟikan tattaḷavum naṭattuṅ kīrttit
teṉṉūṟṟu malaimarutap paṉcolaccaṅ karanamacci vāyaṉ ṟāṉē.

((potuppāyiram))

((pāyirattiṉ peyarkaḷ))

NA_ca_1

mukavurai patika maṇinturai nūṉmukam
puṟavurai tanturai puṉainturai pāyiram.

eṉpatu cūttiram. eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, pāyirattiṟku varum kāraṇakkuṟikaḷ uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pāyirattiṉilakkaṇaṅkaḷai muṉṉuṇarntallatu nūlkaḷaic ceytalum avaṟṟai ītalum ēṟṟalum muṭiyāmai karuti muṉṉuraittaliṉ mukavuraiyeṉṟum, "patikak kiḷavi palvakaip poruḷait, tokuti yākac colluta ṟāṉē" eṉpa vākaliṉ mēlvakukkum aintupotuvum patiṉoru ciṟappumākiya palvakaipporuḷaiyum tokuttuc collutaliṟ patikameṉṟum, nūliṉatu perumai mutaliya viḷaṅka aṇinturaittaliṉ aṇinturai yeṉṟum, mukavuraiyeṉṟatupōla nūṉmukameṉṟum, nūlnutaliyaporu ḷallaṉavaṟṟai uraittaliṟ puṟavuraiyeṉṟum, nūṟkuḷḷa nutaliya poruḷallaṉavaṟṟai ataṟkut tanturaittaliṉ tanturai yeṉṟum, aṇinturai yeṉṟāṟ pōlap puṉainturaiyeṉṟum pāyirattiṟkup peyarām e-ṟu.

pāyirameṉpatu varalāṟu. pāyiraṅkūṟap pukuntavar nikaṇṭupōla ataṉ peyarvikaṟpaṅkaḷaik kūṟiyatu eṉṉai yeṉiṉ, ippāyiram patikamākiya puṟavuraiyāyt tanturaikkappaṭuvatēṉum nūṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāta aṇiyāy muṉ uraikkappaṭuvateṉpatu ikkāraṇakkuṟikaḷāṉ viḷaṅkutaliṉeṉka.

((pāyira vakai.))

NA_ca_2

pāyiram potucciṟap peṉaviru pāṟṟē.

e-ṉ, mēṟ pāyirameṉṟataṉai vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟpāyirameṉṟatu, potuppāyiramum ciṟappuppāyiramumeṉa iruvakaiyiṉaiyuṭaittu e-ṟu.

((potuppāyirattilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_3

nūlē nuvalvō ṉuvalun tiṟaṉē
koḷvōṉ kōṭaṟ kūṟṟā maintum
ellā nūṟku mivaipotup pāyiram.

e-ṉ, iruvakaip pāyirattuṭ potuppāyirattai virittuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūliṉatu varalāṟum āciriyaṉatu varalāṟum avvāciriyaṉ nūlai māṇākkaṟkuc collutaliṉ varalāṟum māṇākkaṉatu varalāṟum avaṉ kēṭṭaliṉ varalāṟum eṉṉum aintum ellā nūṟkum āmātaliṉ, ivvaintu varalāṟṟaiyum viḷaṅka uṇarttuvatu potuppāyiramām e-ṟu.

tiṟaṉ - kūṟṟu: eṉṟatu varalāṟṟaiyātaliṉ ataṉai ēṉaiyavaṟṟuḷḷum piritturaittām, āmeṉpataṉai ellā nūṟkumāmeṉak kūṭṭuka.
"īvōṉ ṟaṉmai yīta liyaṟkai
koḷvōṉ ṟaṉmai kōṭaṉ marapeṉa
īriraṇ ṭeṉpa potuviṉ ṟokaiyē"

eṉpārumuḷarālōveṉiṉ, pāyiraṅkūṟutal nūlkaṭkaṉṟip piṟavaṟṟiṟkaṉṟē? aṅṅaṉamātaliṉ, nūlkaḷiṉ varalāṟoṉṟumē kūṟavēṇṭum; ataṉōṭu āciriyaṉ varalāṟu mutaliya nāṉkiṉaiyum uṭaṉkūṟutal varalāṟṟu muṟaimaiyiṉ vārātār nūlkaḷaik kaṟpikkavum kaṟkavum pukiṉ,
"poypaṭu moṉṟō puṉaipūṇuṅ kaiyaṟiyāp
pētai viṉaimēṟ koḷiṉ" (kuṟaḷ, 836)

eṉṟavāṟēyāy nūlkaḷarumaiyum perumaiyum keṭṭup payaṉpaṭāvāmeṉpatu karutiyeṉka.

((1. nūliṉ varalāṟu))

NA_ca_4

nūli ṉiyalpē nuvali ṉōriru
pāyiran tōṟṟi mummaiyi ṉoṉṟāy
nāṟporuṭ payattō ṭeḻumatan taḻuvi
aiyiru kuṟṟamu makaṟṟiyam māṭciyō
ṭeṇṇāṉ kuttiyi ṉōttup paṭalam
eṉṉu muṟuppiṉiṟ cūttiraṅ kāṇṭikai
virutti yākum vikaṟpanaṭai peṟumē.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya aintaṉuḷ nūliṉatu varalāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya aintaṉuḷ nūliṉatu varalāṟṟaic colliṉ, iruvakaip pāyirattaiyum muṉṉuṭaittātal mutalāka iṅṅaṉam vikaṟpitta patiṉoru vikaṟpanaṭaikaḷaip peṟṟu varum e-ṟu.

((nūlkaḷiṉ vakai))

NA_ca_5

mutalvaḻi cārpeṉa nūṉmūṉ ṟākum.

e-ṉ, nūliṉatu varalāṟṟait tokuttuk kūṟiṉār, avaṟṟuḷ ōriru pāyirattiṟku vakai pōntu kiṭantamaiyiṉ ataṉai yoḻittu ēṉaiyavaṟṟai vakuttuk kūṟuvāṉ toṭaṅki mummai yiṉoṉṟāy" (cū. 4) eṉṟataṉai vakuttu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mutaṉūl vaḻinūl cārpunūleṉa mūṉṟu kūṟṟatām nūl e-ṟu.

((mutaṉūl))

NA_ca_6

avaṟṟuḷ,
viṉaiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ
muṉaivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum.

e-ṉ, nūl mūṉṟaṉuḷ mutaṉūlāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) viṉaiyiṉ nīkki viḷakkappaṭum aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiya uyirkaṭku viṉaiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiya mutalvaṉ ātikkaṇṇē ceytatu yātu? atu mutaṉūlām e-ṟu.

((vaḻinūl))

NA_ca_7

muṉṉōr nūliṉ muṭiporuṅ kottup
piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅ kūṟi
aḻiyā marapiṉatu vaḻinū lākum.

e-ṉ, vaḻinūlāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) tollāciriyar nūlkaḷiṉ poruṇmuṭipu muḻuvatu mottu vaḻi nūl ceyvōṉ mutaṉūluḷatākavum tāṉ vaḻinūl ceytaṟkuk kāraṇamāka vēṇṭiya vikaṟpaṅkaḷaiyum uṭaṉkūṟi avvikaṟpaṅkaḷ uṇarvuṭaiyōr palarkkum oppa muṭintamaiyiṉ aḻiyātu ulakattu niṉṟu nilavum marapiṉai uṭaiyatu vaḻinūlām e-ṟu.

piṉṉōṉvēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅkūṟalāvatu, paḻaiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum vaḻuvala (cū. 462) veṉṟu avaṟṟai iṟantatu vilakkal, etiratu pōṟṟaleṉṉum uttikaḷāṉ vilakkiyum pōṟṟiyuṅ kūṟutal mutaliyaṉa.

`muṉaivaṉkaṇṭatu mutaṉūlākum' (cū. 6) eṉa orumaiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. īṇṭu muṉṉōr nūleṉap paṉmaiyāṟ kūṟiyatu. iṟaivaṉūlaiyum avaṉaruḷ vaḻippaṭṭut tattamarapiṉvarum kuravar palar nūlaiyum taḻīikkōṭaṟkeṉka. eṉavē mutaṉūl māttiraiyāy niṟpatu iṟaivaṉūlum vaḻinūl māttiraiyāy niṟpatu iṟuti nūlumaṉṟi iṭainiṟkum nūlkaḷellām oruvaṟku maintaṉāyiṉāṉ maṟṟoruvaṟkut tantaiyiṉāṟ pōla mutaṉūlāyum vaḻinūlāyum niṟkumeṉpatu peṟṟām.

((cārpu nūl))

NA_ca_8

iruvar nūṟku moruciṟai toṭaṅkit
tiripuvē ṟuṭaiyatu puṭainū lākum.

e-ṉ, cārpunūlāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mutaṉūl vaḻinūleṉṉum irutiṟattu nūlkaṭkum poruṇmuṭipu orupuṭaiyottu oḻintaṉavellām ovvāmaiyuṭaiyatu cārpunūlām e-ṟu.

eṉavē, mutaṉūṟku vaḻinūlum cārpunūlum oruvaṟku maintaṉum marumāṉum pōlumeṉka. orunūl, taṉakku vaḻi nūlainōkkiṉ mutaṉūlākavum, mutanūlainōkkiṉ vaḻi nūlākavum, ayaṉūlai nōkkiṟ cārpunūlākavum niṟkumāyiṉum immuttiṟattiṉuḷ oru tiṟattiṉkaṇ maṟṟoru tiṟamiṉmaiyiṉ mummaiyiṉoṉṟā yeṉṟār.

((vaḻinūl cārpunūṟ ciṟappuviti))

((veṇpā))

NA_ca_9

muṉṉōr moḻi poruḷē yaṉṟi yavarmoḻiyum
poṉṉēpōṟ pōṟṟuva meṉpataṟkum - muṉṉōriṉ
vēṟunūl ceytumeṉu mēṟkōḷil leṉpataṟkum
kūṟupaḻañ cūttirattiṉ kōḷ.

e-ṉ, vaḻinūṟkum cārpunūṟkum eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu. muṉṉōr nūliṉ poruṇmuṭipu muḻuvatum oppavum orupuṭaiyoppavum kūṟuvataṉṟi annūṟ cūttiraṅkaḷaiyum orōvaḻi eṭuttuk kūṟuka veṉṟaliṉ.

(i-ḷ.) muṉṉōr moḻinta poruḷaiyē yaṉṟi avar moḻiyiṉaiyum poṉpōlap pōṟṟikkoḷva meṉpataṟku ilacciṉaiyākavum muṉṉōr nūlaiyē kūṟātu annūliṉiṉṟum vaḻinūl cārpunūl ceytōmāyiṉum āciriyavacaṉaṅkaḷai orōvaḻi eṭuttu uṭaṉ kūṟutal vaḻi nūṟkum cārpunūṟkum ilakkaṇamātaliṉ innūlakattu āciriyavacaṉamillai yeṉak kuṟṟaṅ kūṟuvārātaliṉ akkuṟṟam oḻitaṟkākavum paḻañ cūttirattiṉ kōḷaik kūṟu e-ṟu.

āciriyavacaṉameṉiṉum mēṟkōḷeṉiṉum paḻañcūttirattiṉ kōḷeṉiṉum okkum. muṉ pōṟṟuvam, ceytumeṉa uḷappāṭṭut taṉmaippaṉmaimuṟṟākavum, piṉṉark kūṟeṉa muṉṉilaiyorumai yēvalākavum kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, vaḻinūlum cārpunūlum ceyvōr palar kuḻīi oruvark koruvar kūṟuṅ kūṟṟāka ic cūttirañ ceyyappaṭṭateṉka.

((nāṟporuṭpayaṉ))

NA_ca_10

aṟamporu ḷiṉpamvī ṭaṭaitaṉūṟ payaṉē.

e-ṉ, nāṟporuṭ payattōṭeṉṟataṉai vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) aṟamutaliya nāṉkiṉaiyum ōtiṉōraṭaital nūlāṉ varum payaṉām e-ṟu.

((eḻu matam))

NA_ca_11

eḻuvakai matamē yuṭaṉpaṭaṉ maṟuttal
piṟartam matamēṟ koṇṭu kaḷaivē
tāa ṉāṭṭit taṉātu niṟuppē
iruvar māṟukō ḷorutalai tuṇivē
piṟarnūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭa lēṉaip
piṟitoṭu paṭāaṉ ṟaṉmataṅ koḷalē.

e-ṉ, eḻumatameṉṟataṉai vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēl eḻuvakai matameṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa iṅṅaṉam piṟarmatattai uṭaṉpaṭal mutaliyavāka vitantaṉavām e-ṟu.

nūltaḻuviya mataṅkaḷ palavāyiṉum talaimaipaṟṟi matamēḻeṉpatu nūl vaḻakkāmeṉka.

kuṟṟam pattu, aḻuku pattu, utti muppattiraṇ ṭeṉpaṉavum aṉṉa.

ivveḻuvakai mataṅkaṭkum patapporuḷōṭu utāraṇam viḷaṅka innūluḷ vantuḻi vantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka.

((kuṟṟam pattu))

NA_ca_12

kuṉṟak kūṟaṉ mikaipaṭak kūṟal
kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟal
uccoṟ puṇarttaṉ mayaṅka vaittal
veṟṟeṉat toṭuttaṉ maṟṟoṉṟu virittal
ceṉṟutēyn tiṟuta ṉiṉṟupaya ṉiṉmai
eṉṟivai yīraiṅ kuṟṟa nūṟkē.

e-ṉ, pattuk kuṟṟameṉpataṉai vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūṟkup pattuk kuṟṟaṅkaḷāvaṉa kuṉṟak kūṟal mutaliyavaikaḷām e-ṟu.

nūlkaḷ ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷ cārātu varutal kāṇka.

((aḻaku pattu))

NA_ca_13

curuṅkac collal viḷaṅka vaittal
naviṉṟōrk kiṉimai naṉmoḻi puṇarttal
ōcai yuṭaimai yāḻamuṭait tātal
muṟaiyiṉ vaippē yulakamalai yāmai
viḻumiyatu payattal viḷaṅkutā raṇatta
tākuta ṉūliṟ kaḻakeṉum pattē.

e-ṉ, pattaḻakeṉṟataṉai vakattuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūliṟku aḻakeṉṟu collappaṭum pattāvaṉa curuṅkac collal mutaliyavām e-ṟu.

nūlkaḷ ippattaḻakōṭuṅ kūṭivarutal kāṇka.

((utti muppattiraṇṭu))

NA_ca_14

nutalip pukuta lōttumuṟai vaippē
tokuttuc cuṭṭal vakuttuk kāṭṭal
muṭittuk kāṭṭaṉ muṭiviṭaṅ kūṟal
tāṉeṭuttu moḻital piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal.
5 coṟporuḷ viritta ṟoṭarccoṟ puṇarttal
iraṭṭuṟa moḻita lētuviṉ muṭittal
oppiṉ muṭittaṉ māṭṭeṟin toḻukal
iṟantatu vilakka letiratu pōṟṟal
muṉmoḻintu kōṭal piṉṉatu niṟuttal
10 vikaṟpattiṉ muṭittaṉ muṭintatu muṭittal
uraittu meṉṟa luraittā meṉṟal
orutalai tuṇita leṭuttuk kāṭṭal
eṭutta moḻiyi ṉeyta vaittal
iṉṉa talla tituveṉa moḻital
15 eñciya colli ṉeytak kūṟal
piṟanūṉ muṭinnatu tāṉuṭaṉ paṭutal
taṉkuṟi vaḻakka mikaveṭut turaittal
colliṉ muṭivi ṉapporuṇ muṭittal
oṉṟiṉa muṭitta ṟaṉṉiṉa muṭittal
20 uyttuṇara vaippeṉa vuttiyeṇ ṇāṉkē.

e-ṉ, muppattiraṇṭu uttiyeṉṟataṉai vakuttuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) muppattiraṇṭuttiyāvaṉa nutalippukutal mutaliyavām e-ṟu.

ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟiṉamuṭittal taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉpatu ōr utti; uyttuṇaravaippeṉpataṉai uttikku aṭaiyākki uyttuṇaravaippeṉum utti eṇṇāṉkēyeṉap pāṭamōti ataṉai iraṇṭuttiyākkiṉumamaiyum.

ivvuttikaṭkup patapporuḷōṭu utāraṇam viḷaṅka innūluḷ vantuḻi vantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka. matattiṉuḷḷum aḻakiṉuḷḷum varuvaṉa cilavaṟṟai uttiyuḷḷum kūṟiyatu eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, koḷkai vakaiyāṉ matameṉṟum ciṟappu vakaiyāṉ aḻakeṉṟum immatam aḻaku mutaliyavellām putti nuṭpattu amaiyum vakaiyāṉ uttiyeṉṟum kūṟappaṭumeṉka. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, matam aḻakellāvaṟṟaiyum uttiyiṉpāṟpaṭuttik kūṟātu cilavaṟṟaik kūṟiyatu eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, varampiṉṟi varum uttiyuḷ talaimaipaṟṟik kūṟum muppattiraṇṭil vantaṉa kūṟiṉāreṉka.

((utti iṉṉateṉpatu))

NA_ca_15

nūṟporuḷ vaḻakkoṭu vāyppak kāṭṭi
ēṟpuḻi yaṟintitaṟ kivvakai yāmeṉat
takumvakai celuttuta ṟantira vutti.

e-ṉ, mēl tokuttum vakuttuṅ kūṟiya uttikku ilakkaṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ōr ilakkaṇanūlāl uṇarttappaṭum poruḷai nūlvaḻakkōṭum ulakavaḻakkōṭum porunta uṇartti, apporuḷai maṟṟōr ilakkiya nūṟkaṇṇum ēṟkumiṭam aṟintu ivviṭattiṟku iḵtāmeṉakkarutit takkavakaiyākac celuttutal tantiravuttiyām e-ṟu.

tantirameṉpatu nūl uttiyāṉ amaikkum poruḷai uttiyeṉṟatu ākupeyar.

((ōttilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_16

nēriṉa maṇiyai niralpaṭa vaittāṅ
kōriṉap poruḷai yoruvaḻi vaippa
tōtteṉa moḻipa vuyarmoḻip pulavar.

e-ṉ, iruvakaiyuṟuppiṉuḷ ōttuṟuppāmāṟuṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) oru cātiyāyuḷḷa maṇikaḷai muṟaiyē patittāṟ pōla orucātiyāyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷai oruvaḻippaṭak kūṟuvatu ōttuṟuppāmeṉac colluvar, uyirkku uṟuti payakkum meymmoḻikaḷaiyuṭaiya pulavar e-ṟu.

nērtal - oṉṟupaṭutal. ivvāṟu varutal innūluṟuppiṉuṭ kāṇka.

((paṭalavilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_17

oruneṟi yiṉṟi viraviya poruḷāṟ
potumoḻi toṭari ṉatupaṭala mākum.

e-ṉ, paṭalavuṟuppāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) oruvaḻippaṭātu virāyaporuḷoṭu poruntip pala poruḷai yuṇarttum potuc coṟkaḷ orōvaḻiyiṉṟit toṭarntuvariṉ atu paṭalavuṟuppām e-ṟu.

paṭalavuṟuppiṉaiyuṭaiya kāppiyaṅkaḷuḷ pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉatu caritaiyēyaṉṟi malai kaṭal nāṭu mutaliya palaporuṭ ṭiṟaṅkaḷum viravivarutalum pala poruḷaiyuṇarttum potuc coṟkaḷ orōvaḻiyaṉṟit toṭarntuvarutalum kāṇka.

((cūttiravilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_18

cilavakai yeḻuttiṟ palvakaip poruḷaic
cevva ṉāṭiyiṟ ceṟittiṉitu viḷakkit
tiṭpa nuṭpañ ciṟantaṉa cūttiram.

e-ṉ, cūttiramāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ciṟiya kaṇṇāṭiyiṟ periya yākkai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ cāyai cevvākac ceṟintu iṉitāka viḷaṅkiṉāṟpōlac cevvākac ceṟintu iṉitāka viḷaṅkac cilvakai yeḻuttukkaḷāṉ iyaṉṟa yāppiṉkaṭ palavakaippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaic cevvākac ceṟittu iṉitāki viḷakki appalaporuṭṭiṇmaiyum nuṇmaiyum ciṟantuvaravaṉa cūttiraṅkaḷām e-ṟu.

tiṭpam - kuṟṟamiṉmaiyiṉ alaivaṟa niṟṟal. ivvāṟu varutal iccūttirattuḷḷuṅ kāṇka.

((cūttira nilai))

NA_ca_19

āṟṟoḻuk karimā nōkkan tavaḷaip
pāyttup paruntiṉvīḻ vaṉṉacūt tiranilai.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarntu niṟkum nilaikaḷ āṟṟoḻukku mutaliya nāṉkiṉaiyum pōlum e-ṟu.

ivvāṟu niṟṟal innūluḷ vantuḻi vantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka.

((cūttira vakai))

NA_ca_20

piṇṭan tokaivakai kuṟiyē ceykai
koṇṭiyal puṟaṉaṭaik kūṟṟaṉa cūttiram.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) piṇṭameṉavum, tokaiyeṉavum, vakaiyeṉavum, kuṟiyeṉavum, ceykaiyeṉavum ivaṟṟai alaivaṟak koṇṭu ivaṟṟiṉ puṟattu aṭaiyāy varum puṟaṉaṭaiyeṉavum kūṟum kūṟṟiṉaiyuṭaiyaṉavām, mēṟkūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷ e-ṟu.

ivaṟṟuṭ piṇṭamāvaṉa: "paṉṉirupāṟ ṟatuvē" (cū. 57) eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattut tokaipōlātu, "naṉkiyampuvaṉeḻuttē" (cū. 56) eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattup potuppaṭavaruvaṉa. tokaiyum vakaiyum puṟaṉaṭaiyum vantuḻivantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka. viri vakaiyiṉpāṟ paṭutaliṟ kūṟārāyiṉar. kuṟiyāvaṉa: ivai uyir, ivai oṟṟu, ivai peyar, ivai viṉai eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattu aṟitaṉ māttiraiyāy varuvaṉa. kuṟiyeṉpatu aṟitalaiyuṇarttiya mutaṉilait toḻiṟ peyar. ceykaiyāvaṉa: patamuṉ vikutiyum patamu murupum puṇarum puṇarcci vitiyaṟintu aṅṅaṉamaṟitaṉ māttiraiyāy nillātu avvāṟu vēṇṭuḻip puṇarttalaic ceytalum, peyar viṉai mutaliya koḷḷum muṭipu vitiyaṟintu aṅṅaṉam aṟitaṉ māttiraiyāy nillātu avvāṟu vēṇṭuḻi vēṇṭuḻi muṭittalaicceytalu mutaliyaṉa. ippiṇṭam mutaliya āṟu peyarum apporuḷaiyuṇarttuñ cūttiraṅkaṭku ākupeyarāy vantaṉa. ellāc cūttiraṅkaḷum piṇṭam, tokai, vakai, puṟaṉaṭaiyeṉṉum nāṉkaṉuḷ aṭaṅkumaṉṟō? aṅṅaṉamākak kuṟiyiṉaiyum ceykaiyiṉaiyum vēṟōtiyatu, ellā nūluḷḷum collappaṭuvaṉa aṟivatum ceyvatumiṉṟi vēṟiṉmaiyiṟ piṇṭamākkiyum tokuttum vakuttum puṟattaṭai koṭuttum kūṟappaṭuvaṉa kuṟiyum ceykaiyumē yeṉpata tōṉṟaṟkeṉka. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟavē ellā nūluḷḷum varum ellāc cūttiraṅkaḷum kuṟiccūttiram ceykaic cūttirameṉa iraṇṭā yaṭaṅkumeṉpatu peṟṟām. ivvāṟaṉṟik kuṟiyeṉpataṟkap peyarkaḷai yuṇarttum cūttirameṉṟum, ceykai yeṉpataṟku eḻuttuppuṇarcciyai yuṇarttum cūttira meṉṟum poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar. aṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiṉ, ēṉaiyilakkaṇaṅkaḷaik kūṟum cūttiraṅkaḷaiyum vitantu kūṟavēṇṭum; avai iṉmaiyāṉum peyarum puṇarcciyum ivvilakkaṇa nūṟkaṉṟip potuppāyiramāy ellā nūṟkum poruntāmaiyāṉum atu poruḷaṉṟeṉka.

((uraiyiṉ potuvilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_21

pāṭaṅ karuttē colvakai coṟporuḷ
tokutturai yutāraṇam viṉāviṭai vicēṭam
virivati kāran tuṇivu payaṉō
ṭāciriya vacaṉameṉ ṟīrē ḻuraiyē.

e-ṉ, mēṟ kāṇṭikaiyurai viruttiyurai yeṉṟār, avaṟṟai uṇarttuvāṉṟoṭaṅki, uraiyiṉatu potu vilakkaṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ippatiṉāṉku vakaiyāṉ uraikkappaṭum nūṟku urai e-ṟu.

ivaṟṟuḷ, vicēṭamāvatu: cūttirattuṭ poruḷaṉṟi āṇṭaikku vēṇṭuvaṉa tanturaittal; virivāvatu: vēṟṟumai mutaliya tokku niṉṟaṉavaṟṟai virikkavēṇṭuḻi viritturaittal; atikāramāvatu: eṭuttukkoṇṭa atikāram ituvātaliṉ iccūttirattu atikaritta poruḷ ituveṉa avvatikārattōṭu porunta uraikkavēṇṭuḻi uraittal; tuṇivāvatu: aiyuṟak kiṭantuḻi itaṟku ituvē poruḷeṉa uraittal; ēṉaiya poruḷ viḷaṅkik kiṭantaṉa.

ippatiṉāṉkum ivvuraiyuḷ vantuḻi vantuḻik kāṭṭutum; āṅkāṅkuṇarka.

((kāṇṭikaiyurai))

NA_ca_22

karuttup patapporuḷ kāṭṭu mūṉṟiṉum
avaṟṟoṭu viṉāviṭai yākka lāṉum
cūttirat tuṭporu ṭōṟṟuva kāṇṭikai.

e-ṉ, kāṇṭikaiyuraiyāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya patiṉāṉkiṉuṭ karutturaiyum patapporuḷum utāraṇamumākiya mūṉṟaṉaiyum uraittalāṉum ammūṉṟaṉōṭu viṉā viṭaiyeṉṉum iraṇṭaṉaiyum kūṭṭiyuraittalāṉum cūttirattuṭporuḷai viḷakkuvaṉa kāṇṭikaiyuraikaḷām e-ṟu.

((viruttiyurai))

NA_ca_23

cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyu māṇṭaik
kiṉṟi yamaiyā yāvaiyum viḷaṅkat
taṉṉurai yāṉum piṟarnū lāṉum
aiya makalavaiṅ kāṇṭikai yuṟuppoṭu
meyyiṉai yeñcā ticaippatu virutti.

e-ṉ, viruttiyuraiyāmāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kāṇṭikaiyuraipōṟ cūttirattuṭporuḷ viḷakkutaṉ māttiraiyiṉillātu avviṭaṅkaṭku iṉṟiyamaiyāta poruḷkaḷ yāvaiyum viḷaṅkat tāṉuraikku muraiyāṉum āciriyavacaṉaṅkaḷālum mēṟkūṟiya kāṇṭikaiyuṟuppu aintaṉāṉum aiyantīrac curuṅkātu meymmaipporuḷai viritturaippatu viruttiyuraiyām e-ṟu.

cūttirattuṭporuḷaṉṟiyu meṉṟamaiyāṉ, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉāṉkuṟuppuṅ koṇṭatu viruttiyurai yeṉpatu peṟṟām.

((nūleṉṉum peyarkkāraṇam))

((veṇpā))

NA_ca_24

pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuva liḻaiyākac
ceñcoṟ pulavaṉē cēyiḻaiyā - eñcāta
kaiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
maiyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu.

e-ṉ, nūleṉṉum peyar varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūl muṭiyumāṟu ituvātaliṉ, nūleṉṟatu uvamavākupeyarām e-ṟu.

NA_ca_25

urattiṉ vaḷamperukki yuḷḷiya tīmaip
purattiṉ vaḷamurukkip pollā - marattiṉ
kaṉakkōṭṭan tīrkkunū laḵtēpōṉ māntar
maṉakkōṭṭan tīrkkunūṉ māṇpu.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) annūlpōṟ ceppañceytalāṉum nūleṉṟatu uvamavāku peyarām e-ṟu.

purattiṉatu urattiṉ vaḷam perukki uḷḷiya tīmaivaḷamurukkiyeṉak kūṭṭuka. puram - uṭampu; uram-neñcu. piṉpu tīmaivaḷameṉṟamaiyāṉ muṉpu nalvaḷameṉat tanturaikka; nalvaḷam - aṟivu; tīmaivaḷam - aṟiyāmai.

((2. āciriyariyalpu))

((nallāciriyar))

NA_ca_26

kulaṉaru ṭeyvaṅ koḷkai mēṉmai
kalaipayi ṟeḷivu kaṭṭurai vaṉmai
nilamalai niṟaikōṉ malarnikar māṭciyum
ulakiya laṟivō ṭuyarkuṇa miṉaiyavum
amaipava ṉūlurai yāciri yaṉṉē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē āciriyaṉatu varalāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviyalpiṉaiyuṭaiyār kaṟpikkum āciriyarāvār e-ṟu.

NA_ca_27

terivarum perumaiyun tiṇmaiyum poṟaiyum
paruva muyaṟci yaḷaviṟ payattalum
maruviya naṉṉila māṇpā kummē.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya uvamaiyai virittu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nilattiṉatu māṭcimai ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamaiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_28

aḷakka lākā vaḷavum poruḷum
tuḷakka lākā nilaiyun tōṟṟamum
vaṟappiṉum vaḷantarum vaṇmaiyu malaikkē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) malaiyiṉatu māṭcimai ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamaiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_29

aiyan tīrap poruḷai yuṇarttalum
meynnāṭu nilaiyu miku niṟai kōṟkē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) niṟaikōliṉatu māṭcimai ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamaiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_30

maṅkala māki yiṉṟi yamaiyā
tiyāvaru makiḻntu mēṟkoḷa melkip
poḻutiṉ mukamalar vuṭaiyatu pūvē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) malariṉatu māṭcimai ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamaiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

ivvuvamaikaḷiṉatu māṭcimaippōlap poruḷkaḷiṉatu māṭcimaiyaiyum virittuk koḷka.

((āciriyarallātavar))

NA_ca_31

moḻikuṇa miṉmaiyu miḻikuṇa viyalpum
aḻukkā ṟavāvañca macca māṭalum
kaḻaṟkuṭa maṭaṟpaṉai paruttik kuṇṭikai
muṭatteṅ koppeṉa muraṇkoḷ cintaiyum
uṭaiyō rilarā ciriyarā kutalē.

e-ṉ, āciriyarākār ivareṉa uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyōr kaṟpikkum āciriyarākār e-ṟu.

NA_ca_32

peytamuṟai yaṉṟip piṟaḻavuṭaṉ ṟarum
ceyti kaḻaṟpey kuṭattiṉ cīrē.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya uvamaiyai virittu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kaḻaṟpeyta kuṭattiṉ kuṟṟam ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamaiyāyiṟṟu.

NA_ca_33

tāṉē tarakkoḷi ṉaṉṟit taṉpāl
mēvik koḷakkoṭā viṭattatu maṭaṟpaṉai.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) maṭaṟpaṉaiyiṉ kuṟṟam ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamaiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_34

aritiṟ peyakkoṇ ṭapporu ṭāṉpiṟark
keḷitī villatu paruttik kuṇṭikai.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) paruttipeyta kuṇṭikaiyiṉ kuṟṟam ituvātaliṉ ītu uvamaiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

NA_ca_35

palvakai yutavi vaḻipaṭu paṇpiṉ
allōrk kaḷikku matumuṭat teṅkē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) muṭatteṅkiṉatu kuṟṟam ituvātaliṉ, iḵtu uvamaiyāyiṟṟu e-ṟu.

ivvuva maikaḷiṉ kuṟṟampōlap poruḷkaḷiṉ kuṟṟamum viritturaittukkoḷka.

3. pāṭañcollutaliṉ ilakkaṇam

NA_ca_36

īta liyalpē yiyampuṅ kālaik
kālamu miṭaṉum vālitiṉ nōkkic
ciṟantuḻi yiruntutaṉ ṟeyvam vāḻtti
uraikkap paṭumporu ḷuḷḷat tamaittu
viraiyāṉ vekuḷāṉ virumpi mukamalarntu
koḷvōṉ koḷvakai yaṟintavaṉuḷaṅ koḷak
kōṭṭamiṉ maṉattiṉūl koṭutta leṉpa.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē āciriyaṉ kaṟpikkumāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kaṟpikkumāṟu ivvāṟeṉac colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

4. māṇākkar

NA_ca_37

taṉmaka ṉācāṉ makaṉē maṉmakaṉ
poruṇaṉi koṭuppōṉ vaḻipaṭu vōṉē
uraikōḷa ḷāḷaṟ kuraippatu nūlē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē māṇākkaṉatu varalāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvaṟuvarkkum collappaṭuvatu nūl e-ṟu.

((māṇākkar vakai))

NA_ca_38

aṉṉa māvē maṇṇoṭu kiḷiyē
illik kuṭamā ṭerumai neyyari
aṉṉar talaiyiṭai kaṭaimā ṇākkar.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya aṟuvaruḷ aṉṉamum pacuvum pōlvār talaimāṇākkar: maṇṇum kiḷiyum pōlvār iṭaimāṇākkar; illikkuṭamum āṭum erumaiyum neyyariyum pōlvār kaṭaimāṇākkar e-ṟu.

ivvuvamaittiṟamum poruṭṭiṟamum viritturaittukkoḷka.

((kaṟpikkappaṭātōr))

NA_ca_39

kaḷi maṭi māṉi kāmi kaḷvaṉ
piṇiya ṉēḻai piṇakkaṉ ciṉattaṉ
tuyilvōṉ mantaṉ ṟoṉṉūṟ kañcit
taṭumā ṟuḷattaṉ ṟaṟukaṇaṉ pāvi
paṭiṟaṉiṉ ṉōrkkup pakarār nūlē.

e-ṉ, māṇakkarākārivareṉa uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ikkuṟṟaṅkaḷai yuṭaiyārkku nūlaic collār āciriyar e-ṟu.

5. pāṭaṅkēṭkum muṟai

NA_ca_40

kōṭaṉ marapē kūṟuṅ kālaip
poḻutoṭu ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭaṉ muṉiyāṉ
kuṇattoṭu paḻaki yavaṉkuṟip piṟcārn
tiruveṉa viruntu colleṉac collip
parukuva ṉaṉṉa vārvatta ṉākic
cittirap pāvaiyi ṉattaka vaṭaṅkic
cevivā yāka neñcukaḷa ṉākak
kēṭṭavai kēṭṭavai viṭātuḷat tamaittup
pōveṉap pōta veṉmaṉār pulavar.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē māṇākkaṉ nūlaik kaṟkumāṟu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kaṟkumāṟu ivvāṟeṉac colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

((payiṟci))

NA_ca_41

nūlpayi liyalpē nuvaliṉ vaḻakkaṟital
pāṭam pōṟṟal kēṭṭavai niṉaittal
ācāṟ cārntavai yamaivarak kēṭṭal
ammāṇ puṭaiyōr tammoṭu payiṟal
viṉātal viṉāyavai viṭutta leṉṟivai
kaṭaṉāk koḷiṉē maṭanaṉi yikakkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟu nūlaip payiṉṟavarkku aṟiyāmai mikutiyum pōm e-ṟu.

NA_ca_42

orukuṟi kēṭpō ṉirukāṟ kēṭpiṉ
peruka nūliṟ piḻaipā ṭilaṉē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) orukāṟ kēṭṭa tuṇaiyāṉē amaiyātu irukāṟ kēṭṭāṉāyiṉ annūliṉkaṭ piḻaipāṭu mikutiyum ilaṉ e-ṟu.

NA_ca_43

mukkāṟ kēṭpiṉ muṟaiyaṟin turaikkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mukkāṟ kēṭpāṉāyiṉ āciriyaṉ kaṟpitta muṟaiyaṟinturaippaṉ e-ṟu.

NA_ca_44

ācā ṉuraitta tamaivarak koḷiṉum
kāṟkū ṟallatu paṟṟala ṉākum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) āciriyaṉ kaṟpittaṉavaṟṟait taṉ aṟiviṉkaṇ amaiyak kaṟṟāṉāyiṉum pulamaittiṟattiṟ kāṟkūṟallatu paṟṟāṉ e-ṟu.

NA_ca_45

avviṉai yāḷaroṭu payilvakai yorukāl
cevviti ṉuraippa vavviru kālum
maiyaṟu pulamai māṇpuṭait tākum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) taṉpōliyaroṭu payilumvakaiyāṟ kāṟkūṟum akkalviyait taṉ māṇākkarkkum avaikkaḷattōrkkum uṇara viritturaittalāṉ araikkūṟumākak kuṟṟamaṟṟa pulamai nirampum e-ṟu.

((vaḻipāṭu))

NA_ca_46

aḻali ṉīṅkā ṉaṇukā ṉañci
niḻali ṉīṅkā ṉiṟainta neñcamō
ṭettiṟat tācā ṉuvakku mattiṟam
aṟattiṟ ṟiriyāp paṭarcci vaḻi pāṭē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟoḻukutal vaḻipāṭām e-ṟu.

((ciṟappupā yirattilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_47

ākkiyōṉ peyarē vaḻiyē yellai
nūṟpeyar yāppē nutaliya poruḷē
kēṭpōr payaṉō ṭāyeṇ poruḷum
vāyppak kāṭṭal pāyirat tiyalpē.

e-ṉ, pāyiram potuc ciṟappeṉa (cū. 2) mēl niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē "malartalai yulakiṉ" eṉṟaṟṟoṭakkattavāy ellā nūṟkaṇṇum varum ciṟappuppāyiraṅkaḷiṉatu potuvilakkaṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

itaṉaiyum potuppāyirattuṭ kūṟiṉār, potu aintaṉuḷ nūṉmukattiṉatu ilakkaṇamātaliṉeṉka.

(i-ḷ.) ivveṭṭup poruḷaiyum viḷaṅka uṇarttuvatu ciṟappuppāyirattiṉatu ilakkaṇamām e-ṟu.

ākkiyōṉ peyarē yeṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉavaṟṟuṭ ciṟappeṉpatu tōṉṟiṉamaiyiṟ pāyirameṉa vāḷā kūṟiṉāreṉka.

NA_ca_48

kālaṅ kaḷaṉē kāraṇa meṉṟim
mūvakai yēṟṟi moḻinaru muḷarē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) avveṭṭiṉōṭu immūṉṟiṉaiyuṅ kūṭṭip patiṉoṉṟākak kūṟuvārumuḷar e-ṟu.

((nūṟpeyarvakai))

NA_ca_49

mutaṉūl karutta ṉaḷavu mikuti
poruḷcey vittōṉ ṟaṉmaimuta ṉimittiṉum
iṭukuṟi yāṉunūṟ keytum peyarē.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya patiṉoṉṟaṉuḷ nūṟpeyarkkum yāppiṟkum ciṟappuvitiyuṇarttavēṇṭutaliṉ avviraṇṭaṉuḷ nūṟpeyarkkuc ciṟappuviti uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mutaṉūṉmutal taṉmaiyīṟākak kūṟiya ēḻum piṟavumākiya kāraṇaṅkaḷālum iṭukuṟiyāṉum nūṟkup peyarvarum e-ṟu.

varalāṟu:- mutaṉūlāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa: āriyappaṭalam, pāratamutalāyiṉa; karuttaṉāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa: akattiyam, tolkāppiya mutalāyiṉa; aḷaviṉāṟ peṟṟaṉa: paṉṉirupaṭalam, nālaṭināṉūṟu mutalāyiṉa; mikutiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa: kaḷaviyal mutalāyiṉa; poruḷāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa: akapporuḷ mutalāyiṉa; ceyvittōṉāṟ peyarpeṟṟaṉa: cātavākaṉam, iḷantiraiya mutalāyiṉa; taṉmaiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa cintāmaṇi, naṉṉūl mutalāyiṉa; iṭukuṟiyāṟ peyar peṟṟaṉa: nikaṇṭu, kalaikkōṭṭuttaṇṭu mutalāyiṉa; piṟavumaṉṉa.

((nūlyāppu))

NA_ca_50

tokuttal viritta ṟokaiviri moḻipeyarp
peṉattaku nūlyāp pīriraṇ ṭeṉpa.

e-ṉ, yāppiṉukkuc ciṟappuviti uṇarttuta ṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) nūlyāppu innāṉku kūṟṟatā meṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

veṇpāmutaliya yāppiṉōṭu ivaṟṟiṟku vēṟṟumai tōṉṟa nūlyāppeṉṟār.

((ciṟappuppāyirañ ceytaṟkuriyōr))

NA_ca_51

taṉṉā ciriyaṉ ṟaṉṉoṭu kaṟṟōṉ
taṉmā ṇākkaṉ takumurai kāraṉeṉ
ṟiṉṉōr pāyira miyamputal kaṭaṉē.

e-ṉ, ivvilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya ciṟappuppāyiram kūṟutaṟ kuriyār ivareṉa uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) oruvaṉ kūṟiya nūṟku innālvaruḷoruvar ciṟappuppāyiram kūṟutal muṟaimai e-ṟu.

ciṟappuppāyiram piṟarāṟ ceyyappaṭutaṟkuk kāraṇam

NA_ca_52

tōṉṟā tōṟṟit tuṟaipala muṭippiṉum
tāṉṟaṟ pukaḻta ṟakuti yaṉṟē.

e-ṉ, ciṟappuppāyiram piṟar kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) tōṉṟāta nuṭpaṅkaḷellām tōṟṟip pala tuṟaippaṭṭu virintanūlaic ceytumuṭittāṉāyiṉum taṉṉait tāṉ pukaḻtal takutiyaṉṟāmātaliṉ, nūl ceytāṉatu pukaḻākiya ciṟappuppāyirattaip piṟar kūṟavēṇṭum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_53

maṉṉuṭai maṉṟat tōlait tūkkiṉum
taṉṉuṭai yāṟṟa luṇarā riṭaiyiṉum
maṉṉiya vavaiyiṭai velluṟu poḻutiṉum
taṉṉai maṟutalai paḻitta kālaiyum
taṉṉaip pukaḻtalun takumpula vōrkkē.

e-ṉ, taṟpukaḻcciyum orōvaḻik kuṟṟamaṉṟeṉpatu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviṭaṅkaḷāyiṉ, taṉṉaip pukaḻtalum takutiyām pulavōrkku e-ṟu.

pukaḻtalumeṉṟa ummaiyāṉ ivviṭaṅkaḷilum taṉṉaip pukaḻāmaiyē takutiyeṉpatu peṟṟām.

((pāyiram nūlukku iṉṟiyamaiyātateṉpatu))

NA_ca_54

āyira mukattā ṉakaṉṟa tāyiṉum
pāyira millatu paṉuva laṉṟē.

e-ṉ, iruvakaip pāyiraṅkaḷum nūṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa veṉpatu uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) āyiram uṟuppukkaḷāṉ virinta nūlāyiṉum pāyiramillātatu nūlaṉṟu e-ṟu.

((veṇpā))

NA_ca_55

māṭakkuc cittiramu mānakarkkuk kōpuramum
āṭamaittō ṇallārk kaṇiyumpōl nāṭimuṉ
aituraiyā niṉṟa vaṇinturaiyai yennūṟkum
peyturaiyā vaittār peritu.

e-ṉ, mēṟpāyiramillatu paṉuvalaṉṟeṉṟār, aḵtu aṉṟātaṟkuk kāraṇam uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) aṟivuṭaiyōr māṭamutaliya mūṉṟaṟkum cittira mutaliya mūṉṟumpōlak karuti aḻakiyatākiya poruḷai uṇarttāniṉṟa iruvakaippāyiraṅkaḷaiyu muraittu evvakaippaṭṭa periya nūlkaṭkum muṉṉarp peytuvaittār perumpālum; ātaliṟ pāyiramillatu paṉuvalaṉṟām e-ṟu.

((mutalāvatu eḻuttatikāram))

((mutalāvatu eḻuttiyal))

((kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkamum atikāramum))

NA_ca_56

pūmali yacōkiṉ puṉainiḻa lamarnta
nāṉmukaṟ ṟoḻutunaṉ kiyampuva ṉeḻuttē.

e-ṉ, kaṭavuḷ vaṇakkamum atikāramum uṇarttutaṉutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pūkkaḷ malinta acōkamarattiṉatu alaṅkarikkum niḻaliṉkaṇ eḻuntaruḷiyirunta nāṉku tirumukaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya kaṭavuḷai vaṇaṅki naṉṟākac colluvaṉ eḻuttilakkaṇattai e-ṟu.

ellā nūlum maṅkalamoḻi mutalvakuttuk kūṟavēṇṭutaliṟ pūmaliyeṉṟum, ellāc camayattōrālum vaṇaṅkappaṭum paṭaippumutaliya aintoḻiṟkumuriya ellākkaṭavuḷākiyum niṉṟāṉoruvaṉē yeṉpār arukaṉai nāṉmakaṉeṉṟum kūṟiṉār. ivvāṟu vaḷḷuvanāyaṉārum.
"malarmikai yēkiṉāṉ māṇaṭi cērntār
nilamicai nīṭuvāḻ vār" (kuṟaḷ, 3)

eṉa varaiyātu kūṟutal kāṇka. eḻutteṉpatu ākupeyar; ēkāram īṟṟacai. ellāmvalla kaṭavuḷai vaṇaṅkalāṉ iṉitu muṭiyumeṉpatu karuti naṉkiyampuvaṉeṉṟu pukuntamaiyiṉ itu nutalippukutaleṉṉum utti.

((eḻuttilakkaṇa vakai))

NA_ca_57

eṇpeyar muṟaipiṟap puruva māttirai
mutalī ṟiṭainilai pōli yeṉṟā
patampuṇar peṉappaṉ ṉirupāṟ ṟatuvē.

e-ṉ, avveḻuttilakkaṇam ittuṇaitteṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ippaṉṉirupakutiyiṉaiyu muṭaittu avveḻuttilakkaṇam e-ṟu.

eṉṟāveṉpatu eṇṇiṭaiccol. iccūttiram tokuttuc cuṭṭaleṉṉum utti; mēlvaruvaṉavellām vakuttuk kāṭṭal.

1. eḻuttiṉ eṇ

NA_ca_58

moḻimutaṟ kāraṇa māmaṇut tiraḷoli
eḻuttatu mutalcār peṉaviru vakaittē.

e-ṉ, "paṉṉiru pāṟṟatuvē" (cū. 57) eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ, eḻuttiṉatu akattilakkaṇamākiya pattaiyum ōriyalākavum, ataṉ puṟattilakkaṇamākiya patam puṇarpeṉṉumiraṇṭaṉuḷ avveḻuttāṉākum patattai ōriyalākavum, appatam puṇarum puṇarppai mūṉṟiyalākavum ōttumuṟaivaippeṉṉum uttiyāṉvaikkappukuntu mutaṟkaṇ vaitta eḻuttiyalil eḻuttiṉatu eṇṇiṉai niṟutta muṟaiyāṉ uṇarttuvāṉṟoṭaṅki eḻuttu iṉṉateṉpatūum ataṉ vakaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇamāy aṇuttiraḷiṉ kāriyamāy varum oliyāvatu; eḻuttu; atu mutaleḻutteṉṟum cārpeḻutteṉṟum iruvakaiyiṉai uṭaittu e-ṟu.

eṉavē moḻikku mutaṟkāraṇam eḻuttāṉāṟpōla eḻuttukku mutaṟkāraṇam aṇuttiraḷeṉpatu peṟṟām. āmeṉṉum peyareccam oliyeṉṉum peyaroṭu muṭintatu. muṟku vīḷai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku mutaṟkāraṇamāy aṇuttiraḷiṉ kāriyamāy varumoli eḻuttākāmaiyiṉ, moḻi mutaṟkāraṇamām oliyeṉṟār. citalatu nīrvāyc ciṟu tukaḷāṟ perumpuṟṟuruvamainta peṟṟiya teṉṉa aimpulap pēruruvaintum aintaṇuvāl impariṟ camaiva tiyāvaru maṟitaliṉ, anāti kāraṇamākiya māyaiyiṉai īṇṭak kūṟātu ātikāraṇamākiya cevippulaṉām aṇuttiraḷai eḻuttiṟku mutaṟkāraṇameṉṟār. ivvāciriyarkku māyai uṭaṉpāṭaṉṟu; aṇuttiraḷoṉṟumē tuṇiveṉiṉ, piṟitoṭupaṭāṉ taṉmataṅ koḷaleṉṉum matampaṭak kūṟiṉāreṉṟuṇarka īṇṭu aṇuveṉṟatu oliyiṉatu nuṭpattai.

((mutaleḻuttu))

NA_ca_59

uyiru muṭampumā muppatu mutalē.

e-ṉ, mutaleḻuttiṉ viri uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) uyirum uṭampumākum muppateḻuttum mutaleḻuttām e-ṟu.

((cārpeḻuttu))

NA_ca_60

uyirmey yāyta muyiraḷa poṟṟaḷa
paḵkiya iu aiau maḵkāṉ
taṉinilai pattuñ cārpeḻut tākum.

e-ṉ, cārpeḻuttiṉ viri uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ippattum cārpeḻuttām e-ṟu.

aḵkutal - curuṅkutal. taṉinilai - āytam. uyirkaḷōṭum meykaḷōṭuṅ kūṭiyum kūṭātum alipōlat taṉi niṟṟaliṉ taṉinilaiyeṉappaṭum. uyirmey, uyirummeyyum kūṭippiṟattalāṉum, āytam uyirpōla,
"aṟṟā laḷavaṟin tuṇka vaḵtuṭampu
peṟṟā ṉeṭituykku māṟu" (kuṟaḷ. 943)

eṉa alaku peṟṟum, meypōla,

"tōṉṟiṟ pukaḻoṭu tōṉṟuka vaḵtilār
tōṉṟaliṟ ṟōṉṟāmai naṉṟu" (kuṟaḷ, 236)

eṉa alaku peṟātum orupuṭai yottu avaṟṟiṉ iṭaiyē cārntuvarutalāṉum, ēṉaiya tattam mutaleḻuttiṉ tiripu vikārattāṟ piṟattalāṉum cārpeḻuttāyiṉa veṉak koḷka.

((cārpeḻuttiṉ viri))

NA_ca_61

uyirmey yiraṭṭunūṟ ṟeṭṭuya rāytam
eṭṭuyi raḷapeḻu mūṉṟoṟ ṟaḷapeṭai
āṟē ḻaḵku mimmup pāṉēḻ
ukara māṟā ṟaikāṉ mūṉṟē
aukā ṉoṉṟē maḵkāṉ mūṉṟē
āyta miraṇṭoṭu cārpeḻut tuṟuviri
oṉṟoḻi munnūṟ ṟeḻupā ṉeṉpa.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) uyirmey irunūṟṟuppatiṉāṟu; kuṟukāta āytam eṭṭu; uyiraḷapeṭai irupattoṉṟu; oṟṟaḷapeṭai nāṟpattiraṇṭu; kuṟṟiyalikaram muppattēḻu; kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟu; aikārakkuṟukkam mūṉṟu; aukārak kuṟukkam oṉṟu; makarakkuṟukkam mūṉṟu; āytakkuṟukkam iraṇṭuṭaṉē cārpeḻuttiṉatu mikunta viri munnūṟṟaṟupattoṉpatāmeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

ivai ittuṇaiyavātal piṟappatikārattiṉuṭ kāṇka. ivvāṟu uyirmey oḻintaṉavaṟṟaiyum virittal toṉṉeṟiyeṉpār, eṉpaveṉṟār.

((peyarkaḷiṉ potuvilakkaṇam))

NA_ca_62

iṭukuṟi kāraṇap peyarpotuc ciṟappiṉa.

e-ṉ, niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē eḻuttiṉ peyarāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉṟoṭaṅkip peyarkkellām potuvilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) iṭukuṟippeyarum kāraṇappeyarumākiya iraṇṭum pala poruṭkup potuppeyarākiyum oru poruṭkuc ciṟappup peyarākiyum varuvaṉavām e-ṟu.

oru poruḷaik kuṟittaṟkuk kaṭavuḷāṉum aṟivuṭaiyōrāṉum iṭṭakuṟiyākiya peyar iṭukuṟippeyar; kāraṇattāṉ varumpeyar kāraṇappeyar.

(u-m.) marameṉpatu iṭukuṟippotuppeyar. paṉaiyeṉpatu iṭukuṟic ciṟappuppeyar. aṇiyeṉpatu kāraṇappotuppeyar. muṭiyeṉpatu kāraṇac ciṟappuppeyar.

iṉi iraṭṭuṟamoḻitaleṉṉumuttiyāṉ iccūttirattiṟku iṭukuṟiyeṉṟum kāraṇameṉṟum collappaṭum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya peyarkaḷ iṭukuṟikāraṇameṉṉum iraṇṭaṟkum potuvāyum iṭukuṟikkē ciṟappāyum kāraṇattiṟkē ciṟappāyum varuvaṉavāmeṉṟum poruḷuraittuk koḷka.

(u-m.) marameṉpatu iṭukuṟippeyar. paramaṉeṉṟatu kāraṇappeyar. mukkaṇaṉ, antaṇaṉ, maṟavaṉ, muḷḷi, kaṟaṅku, moḻi, colleṉpaṉa kāraṇa iṭukuṟippeyar. ivai kāraṇaviṭukuṟiyāyateṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, ivaṟṟuḷ mukkaṇaṉeṉṉum peyar āṉaimukakkaṭavuḷ mutaliyōrkkum mūṉṟu kaṇṇuḷavātaliṟ kāraṇaṅkarutiyavaḻi mūṉṟu kaṇṇiṉaiyuṭaiyōr palarkkum cēṟalāṉum kāraṇaṅkarutāvaḻi iṭukuṟi māttiraiyēyāyp paramaṉukkē cēṟalāṉum kāraṇa viṭukuṟiyāyiṟṟu. ēṉaip peyarkaṭkum ivvāṟē kāṇka. ivvāṟē vaṭanūlār iṭukuṟiyai rūṭiyeṉṟum kāraṇattai yōkameṉṟum kāraṇa viṭukuṟiyai yōkarūṭi yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa.

ivvilakkaṇattāṉ eḻuttiṉatu peyarum piṟapeyarum varumāṟu kāṇka.

2. eḻuttiṉ peyar

NA_ca_63

ammuta līrā ṟāvi kammutal
meymmū vāṟeṉa viḷampiṉar pulavar.

e-ṉ, orucāreḻuttiṉatu peyarāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) akaramutal aukāravīṟākak kiṭanta paṉṉiraṇṭiṉaiyum āviyeṉṟum kakaramutalākak kiṭanta patiṉeṭṭiṉaiyum meyyeṉṟum nūlkaḷāṟ coṉṉār aṟivuṭaiyōr e-ṟu.

kaṭavuḷāl āvimeyyeṉṟu amaitta peyarkkāraṇam uyirkaḷāṉ muṟṟum uṇartaṟku arumaiyum kaṭavuṇūl uṇarntōrvaḻic cellum tamatu perumaiyum tōṉṟa viḷampiṉar pulavareṉṟār. āvimeyyumpōṟaliṉ ivviruvakaiyeḻuttiṟkum āvi meyyeṉpaṉa uvamavākupeyarāyk kāraṇappotuppeyarāyiṉa. ēṉaiyavum ivvāṟē kāṇka.

((kuṟil))

NA_ca_64

avaṟṟuḷ
ai ue okkuṟi laintē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) āvi mey eṉṟavaṟṟuḷ ivvaintum kuṟṟeḻuttām e-ṟu.

((neṭil))

NA_ca_65

āī ūē aiō au neṭil.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvēḻum neṭṭeḻuttām e-ṟu.

((cuṭṭeḻuttukkaḷ))

NA_ca_66

ai ummutaṟ ṟaṉivariṟ cuṭṭē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) immūṉṟeḻuttum moḻikkuppuṟattum akattum mutaṟkaṇ taṉittuc cuṭṭup poruḷuṇarttavariṟ cuṭṭeḻuttām e-ṟu.

mutaleṉap potuppaṭakkūṟiṉamaiyiṟ puṟattum akattumeṉpatu peṟṟām.

(u-m.) akkoṟṟaṉ ikkoṟṟaṉ ukkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum, avaṉ ivaṉ uvaṉ eṉavum varum.

avaṉeṉpataṉkaṇ akaram aṟameṉpataṉkaṇ akaram pōlap piṉ eḻuttukkaḷōṭu toṭarntuniṉṟu oruporuḷaiyuṇarttātu malaiyaṉeṉpataṉkaṭ pakutipōla vēṟu niṉṟu cuṭṭupporuḷuṇarttaliṉ akattuvarum itaṉaiyum taṉivariṉeṉṟār. ivvurai viṉāviṟkuṅ koḷka.

((viṉāveḻuttukkaḷ))

NA_ca_67

eyā mutalum āō īṟṟum
ēiru vaḻiyum viṉavā kummē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) puṟattum akattum moḻimutaṟkaṇ ekaramum yāvum, īṟṟiṉkaṇ ākāramum ōkāramum, ivviraṇṭiṭattiṉum ēkāramum taṉittu viṉāpporuḷuṇarttavariṉ viṉāveḻuttām e-ṟu.

mēl taṉivariṉeṉṟataṉai īṇṭum kūṭṭuka. ivai puṟattum akattum varutal ēṟṟapeṟṟi koḷka.

oṉṟiṉamuṭittal taṉṉiṉamuṭittaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ yāviṉāvaiyum uṭaṉkūṟiṉār.

(u-m.) ekkoṟṟaṉ eṉavum evaṉ, yāvaṉ eṉavum, mutaṟkaṇ vantaṉa. koṟṟaṉā, koṟṟaṉō veṉa īṟṟiṉkaṇ vantaṉa. ēvaṉ, koṟṟaṉē eṉa īriṭattum vantaṉa. ēṉaip peyarviṉaikaḷōṭum ēṟṟa peṟṟi oṭṭikkoḷka.

((valliṉam))

NA_ca_68

valliṉaṅ kacaṭata paṟaveṉa vāṟē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟum valliṉamām e-ṟu.

((melliṉam))

NA_ca_69

melliṉam ṅañaṇana maṉaveṉa vāṟē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟum melliṉamām e-ṟu.

((iṭaiyiṉam))

NA_ca_70

iṭaiyiṉam yaralava ḻaḷaveṉa vāṟē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvāṟum iṭaiyiṉamām e-ṟu.

((iṉaveḻuttu))

NA_ca_71

aiau iuc ceṟiya mutaleḻut
tivviraṇ ṭōriṉa māyvaraṉ muṟaiyē.

e-ṉ, iṉamaṭaittal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) iṉamillāta aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ īkāra ūkāraṅkaṭku iṉamākiya ikara ukaraṅkaḷait tamakkum iṉamākap porunta mutaleḻuttukkaḷ ivviraṇṭu ōriṉamāy varutal muṟaiyātalāl avai iṉaveḻutteṉṟum peyar peṟum e-ṟu.

((iṉameṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇam))

NA_ca_72

tāṉa muyaṟci yaḷavu poruḷvaṭi
vāṉavoṉ ṟātiyōr puṭaiyop piṉamē.

e-ṉ, mēl iṉameṉṟataṟkuk kāraṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) tāṉamutaliyavāya ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟumutalāka orupuṭai yottalāl iṉamām e-ṟu.

tāṉam - ura mutaliyaṉa: muyaṟci - itaḻmuyaṟci mutaliyaṉa: aḷavu - māttirai: poruḷ - pālaṉ viruttaṉāṉāṟpōlak kuṟiliṉatu vikāramē neṭilātaliṉ iraṇṭaṟkum poruḷoṉṟeṉṟu mutaṉūlāl niyamikkappaṭṭa poruḷ: vaṭivu - oli vaṭivu, varivaṭivu. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟum palavumottu iṉamāy varutal kaṇṭukoḷka.

cārpeḻuttiṟkup peyar kūṟātoḻintār, āytam taṉi nilaiyātalāṉum ēṉaic cārpeḻuttiṟkuc cuṭṭuviṉāveṉṟu vitantōtiṉavaiyoḻinta mutaleḻuttiṉ peyarē peyarāyaṭaṅkutalāṉu meṉka.

iccūttiram ētuviṉmuṭitta leṉṉum utti.

3. eḻuttiṉ muṟai

NA_ca_73

ciṟappiṉu miṉattiṉuñ ceṟintīṇ ṭammutal
naṭatta ṟāṉē muṟaiyā kummē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē muṟaiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ciṟappiṉāṉum iṉattiṉāṉum porunti ivvulakattu akaramutalāka vaḻaṅkutaṟāṉē eḻuttiṉatu muṟaiyām e-ṟu.

kuṟiliṉatu vikāramē neṭilātalāṟ kuṟil muṉṉiṟṟaliṟ ciṟappiṉeṉṟum, neṭil iṉamāyp piṉṉiṟṟaliṉ iṉattiṉeṉṟum, nātamāttiraiyāy ellā eḻuttiṟkum kāraṇamāy muṉṉiṟṟaliṉ ammutaleṉṟum, ellāveḻuttiṟkum vaitta muṟaikkāraṇam uyirkaḷāṉ muṟṟumuṇartalākāmaiyiṉ naṭattaṟāṉēyeṉṟum kūṟiṉār.

4. eḻuttiṉ piṟappu

NA_ca_74

niṟaiyuyir muyaṟciyi ṉuḷvaḷi turappa
eḻumaṇut tiraḷuraṅ kaṇṭa mucci
mūkkuṟ ṟitaḻnāp pallaṇat toḻiliṉ
ve/vē ṟeḻuttoli yāyvaral piṟappē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē piṟappāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉ toṭaṅki ataṉ potuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) oliyeḻuttākiya kāriyattiṟku vēṇṭum kāraṇaṅkaḷil oruvāṟṟāṉum kuṟaiviṉṟi niṟainta uyiriṉatu muyaṟciyāl uṇṇiṉṟa vaḷiyeḻuppa eḻum cevippulaṉām aṇukkaḷ uramutaliya nāṉkiṭattaiyum porunti itaḻ mutaliya nāṉkiṉ toḻilkaḷāl vevvēṟu eḻuttākiya olikaḷāyt tōṉṟutal avaṟṟiṉ piṟappām e-ṟu.

((mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ iṭappiṟappu))

NA_ca_75

avvaḻi,
āvi yiṭaimai yiṭamiṭa ṟākum
mēvu meṉmaimūk kurampeṟum vaṉmai.

e-ṉ, mutaleḻuttukkaṭku iṭamvakuttal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiyaneṟiyāṟ piṟakkuṅkāl, āvi iṭaiyiṉameṉṉum iruvakai yeḻuttiṉatu iṭam miṭaṟākum; melliṉam mūkkai iṭamākap poruntum; valliṉam neñcai iṭamākap peṟum e-ṟu.

mutaleḻuttukkaḷiṉ muyaṟcip piṟappu

NA_ca_76

avaṟṟuḷ,
muyaṟciyuḷ aā vaṅkāp puṭaiya.

e-ṉ, mutaleḻuttuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku muyaṟci vakaiyāṟ piṟappuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mēl iṭamvakukkappaṭṭa mutaleḻuttukkaḷuḷ, akara ākāraṅkaḷ nālvakai muyaṟciyuḷ aṇṇattiṉ toḻilākiya aṅkāttalai yuṭaiyavāyp piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_77

iī eē aiaṅ kāppō
ṭaṇpaṉ mutaṉā viḷimpuṟa varumē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvainteḻuttum aṅkāppuṭaṉē aṇpallai aṭinā viḷimpu poruntap piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_78

uū oō auvitaḻ kuvivē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivvainteḻuttum piṟattaṟku ētuvāya muyaṟci itaḻkuvivām e-ṟu.

NA_ca_79

kaṅavuñ cañavum ṭaṇavu mutaliṭai
nuṉinā vaṇṇa muṟamuṟai varumē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) kavvum ṅavvum mutaṉā mutalaṇṇattaiyum, cavvum ñavvum iṭainā iṭaiyaṇṇattaiyum, ṭavvum ṇavvum nuṉinā nuṉiyaṇṇattaiyumu/ porunta immuṟaiyē piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_80

aṇpal laṭinā muṭiyuṟat tanavarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṇpallaṭiyai nānuṉi poruntat tavvum navvum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_81

mīkī ḻitaḻuṟap pammap piṟakkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mēlitaḻum kīḻitaḻum poruntap pavvum mavvum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_82

aṭinā vaṭiyaṇa muṟayat tōṉṟum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṭinā aṭiyaṇṇattaip porunta yakaram piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_83

aṇṇa nuṉinā varuṭa raḻavarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṇṇattai nuṉināt taṭava ravvum ḻavvum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_84

aṇpaṉ mutalu maṇṇamu muṟaiyiṉ
nāviḷimpu vīṅki yoṟṟavum varuṭavum
lakāra ḷakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṇpaṉmutalai nāviḷimpu vīṅki oṟṟa lakāramākiyum, aṇṇattai nāviḷimpu vīṅki varuṭa ḷakāramākiyum ivviraṇṭeḻuttum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_85

mēṟpal litaḻuṟa mēviṭum vavvē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mēṟpal kīḻitaḻaip porunta vakāram piṟakkum e-ṟu.

NA_ca_86

aṇṇa nuṉinā naṉiyuṟiṟ ṟaṉavarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) aṇṇattai nuṉinā mikapporuntil ṟavvum ṉavvum piṟakkum e-ṟu.

cārpeḻuttiṉ iṭamum muyaṟciyum

NA_ca_87

āytak kiṭantalai yaṅkā muyaṟci
cārpeḻut tēṉavun tammuta laṉaiya.

e-ṉ, cārpeḻuttukkaṭku iṭamuyaṟci vakaiyāṟ piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) āytam piṟattaṟku iṭam talai; muyaṟci aṅkāttalām; oḻinta cārpeḻuttukkaḷum iṭamuyaṟcikaḷ tam mutaleḻuttukkaḷōṭu oppaṉavāyp piṟakkum e-ṟu.

cārpiṉ cārpākiya āytakkuṟukkamum ataṉ mutal pōlap piṟakkumeṉpār etiratutaḻīi eṉavumeṉṟār.

((piṟappiṉ puṟaṉaṭai))

NA_ca_88

eṭuttal paṭutta ṉalita luḻappiṟ
ṟiripun tattamiṟ ciṟituḷa vākum.

e-ṉ, mēṟkūṟiya piṟappiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) palaveḻuttiṟkum piṟappu oṉṟākac collappaṭṭaṉavēṉum eṭuttal paṭuttal nalitaleṉṉum eḻuttiṟkuriya olimuyaṟciyāṉ oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu piṟappu vēṟupāṭukaḷum avvavaṟṟiṉkaṭ ciṟitu uḷavām e-ṟu.

((uyirmey))

NA_ca_89

puḷḷivaṭ ṭavvoṭu muṉṉuru vākiyum
ēṉai yuyirō ṭuruvu tirintum
uyiraḷa vāyataṉ vaṭivoḻit tiruvayiṟ
peyaroṭu moṟṟumuṉ ṉāyvaru muyirmey.

e-ṉ, mēṟ piṟappuṇarttiya cārpeḻuttiṟkuc cārntu varumāṟum oru piṟappātaliṟ piṟappatikārattuḷ aṭakki uṇarttuvāṉṟoṭaṅki avaṟṟuḷ uyirmey varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mey puḷḷiyai viṭṭu akarattōṭu kūṭiyavaḻi viṭṭa uruvē uruvākiyum oḻinta uyirkaḷōṭu kūṭiya vaḻi uruvuvēṟu paṭṭum taṉmāttirai tōṉṟātu uyirmāttiraiyē māttiraiyāy ataṉ varivaṭiviṉatu vikāravaṭivē vaṭivāy uyir vaṭivai oḻittu meyyuyireṉṉum peyarākiya iraṇṭiṭattum piṟanta uyirmeyyeṉṉum peyaruṭaṉē oṟṟoli muṉṉum uyiroli piṉṉumāki varum uyirmeyyeḻuttu e-ṟu.

oḻitteṉṟum oṟṟumuṉṉāyeṉṟum vanta ciṉaiviṉaikaḷ iruvayiṟ peyaroṭum varumeṉṉum mutalviṉaiyotu muṭintaṉa.

pūti mutaliyavaṟṟiṟṟīṭṭiya varivaṭivum olivaṭivupōlap payaṉṟarum oṟṟumai kuṟittu īṇṭu avvirutiṟaṉum viravik kūṟiṉār.

ivvilakkaṇattāṉ ovvoru meyyiṟ paṉṉīruyiruṅ kūṭap paṉṉirupatiṉeṭ ṭirunūṟṟorupattāṟāy varumāṟu vaḻaṅkum varivaṭivuḷḷum olivaṭivuḷḷum kāṇka.

((muṟṟāytam))

NA_ca_90

kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micaittē.

e-ṉ, muṟṟāytam varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) puḷḷivaṭiviṉatāy āytam kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉatāy uyiroṭu kūṭiya valleḻuttāṟaṉmēlatāy varum e-ṟu.

uyirvarukkattu iṟutikkaṭṭanta āytavaṭivaip piṟkālattu vēṟupaṭa varaintu vaḻaṅkuvarātaliṉ, tollai vaṭivutōṉṟa āytappuḷḷiyeṉṟār.

(u-m.) eḵku, kaḵcu, irupaḵtu, aḵkaṭiya, aḵkāṉ, vaḵkāṉ eṉavum taṉikkuṟileṉṟu varaintōtāmaiyiṉ
"vilaḵḵki vīṅkiru ḷōṭṭumē mātar
ilaḵḵku mutti ṉiṉam"

eṉavum varum. piṟavumaṉṉa.

iccūttiram mēṟkōḷātalāl tāṉeṭuttu moḻitaleṉṉu mutti. valliṉavakaiyāṉ iyalpākavanta āytam āṟaṉōṭu puṇarccivikārattāṉ varumāytamum ceyyuḷ vikārattāṉ varumāytamum kūṭi eṭṭāy varumāṟu kāṇka.

((uyiraḷapeṭai))

NA_ca_91

icaikeṭiṉ moḻimuta liṭaikaṭai nilaineṭil
aḷapeḻu mavaṟṟavaṟ ṟiṉakkuṟil kuṟiyē.

e-ṉ, uyiraḷapeṭai varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ceyyuṭkaṇ icai kuṉṟiṉ moḻimutaliṉum iṭaiyiṉum kaṭaiyiṉum niṉṟa neṭṭeḻuttēḻum avvicai niṟaikkat tam māttiraiyiṉ mikkolikkum: avvāṟu aḷapeṭuttamai aṟitaṟku avaṟṟiṉpiṉ avaṟṟiṟku iṉamākiya kuṟṟeḻuttukkaḷ varivaṭiviṉkaṇ aṟikuṟiyāy varum. e-ṟu. (u-m.)
"āa aḷiya valavaṉṟaṉ pārppiṉō
ṭīi riraiyuṅkoṇ ṭīraḷaip paḷḷiyuṭ
ṭūun tiraiyalaippat tuñcā tiṟaivaṉṟōḷ
mēē valaippaṭṭanampō ṉaṟunutāl
ōovu ḻakkun tuyar"

eṉavum,
"uṟāark kuṟunō yuraippāy kaṭalaic
ceṟāaay vāḻiyaveṉ ṉeñcu" (kuṟaḷ. 1200)

eṉavum,
"aṉiccappūk kālkaḷaiyāḷ peytā ṇucuppiṟku
nalla paṭāa paṟai" (kuṟaḷ. 1115)

eṉavum varum. `ceṟāaay vāḻiya' eṉpuḻi nāṉku māttirai yāyum ēṉaiya mūṉṟumāttiraiyāyum alakupeṟṟuc ceyyuḷicai niṟaittu mūviṭattum vantavāṟu kāṇka. piṟavumaṉṉa.

iṉi icai#eṭiṉeṉṟu potuppaṭak kūṟiṉamaiyāṉ,
"keṭuppatūuṅ keṭṭārkkuc cārvāymaṟ ṟāṅkē
eṭuppatūu mellā maḻai" (kuṟaḷ. 15)

eṉak kuṟilneṭilāy aḷapeḻuntu alakupeṟṟum peṟātum iṉṉicai niṟaippa varuvaṉavum,
"uraṉacaii yuḷḷan tuṇaiyākac ceṉṟār
varaṉacaii yiṉṉu muḷēṉ" (kuṟaḷ, 1263)

eṉa aḷapeḻuntu alakupeṟṟum peṟātum collicai niṟaippa varuvaṉavum.
"vīḻappaṭuvār keḻīiyilar tāḻvīḻvār
vīḻap paṭāa reṉiṉ" (kuṟaḷ. 1194)

eṉpataṉuṭ keḻīiyilareṉa aḷapeḻuntu alaku peṟātu collicai niṟaippa varuvaṉavum koḷka.

eḻuttup palavāyiṉa olivēṟṟumaiyāṉaṉṟē: aṅṅaṉamāka, neṭilatu vikāramāy ōroliyāyp piṟakkum aḷapeṭai iraṇṭeḻuttuk kūṭi mūṉṟumāttiraiyāyiṟṟeṉak koḷḷiṉ, iraṇṭeḻuttoli aṅṅaṉam iṉmaiyāṉum aḷapeṭaiyeṉṉum peyar ēlāmaiyāṉum avvāṟu koḷḷātu aṟikuṟiyēyeṉṟu kōṭaṟkuk kuṟiyēyeṉṟār. ikkaruttēpaṟṟi āciriyar tolkāppiyarum, "(kuṉṟicai moḻivayi ṉiṉṟicai niṟaikku, neṭṭeḻuttu" eṉṟum `avaṟṟiṉpiṉ aṟikuṟiyāy varum ottakuṟṟeḻuttu' eṉṟum kūṟiṉāraṉṟē? kuṟiyē yeṉṟatu, ivviraṇṭu mēṟkōḷil oṉṟu tuṇitalāṉ orutalai tuṇitaleṉṉu muttiyum ammatamumpaṭa vantateṉka.

itaṉuḷ, iṭavakaiyāṉ uyiraḷapeṭai eḻumūṉṟāy varumāṟu kāṇka.

((oṟṟaḷapeṭai))

NA_ca_92

ṅañaṇa namaṉa vayalaḷa vāytam
aḷapāṅ kuṟiliṇai kuṟiṟkī yiṭaikaṭai
mikalē yavaṟṟiṉ kuṟiyām vēṟē.

e-ṉ, oṟṟaḷapeṭai varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ceyyuṭkaṇ icaikuṉṟiṉ, kuṟiliṇaik kīḻum kuṟiṟkīḻumāy moḻiyiṭaiyiṉum īṟṟiṉum niṉṟa ippat toṟṟum āytamum avvōcaiyai niṟaikkat tam māttiraiyiṉ mikkolikkum; ivvāṟu aḷapeṭuttaṉa aṟitaṟku avaṟṟiṉpiṉ avveḻuttukkaḷē varivaṭiviṉkaṇ vēṟu aṟikuṟiyāy varum e-ṟu.

"orumoḻi yoḻitaṉ ṉiṉaṅkoḷaṟ kurittē" (cū. 358) eṉpataṉāl mēl icaikeṭiṉeṉṟataṉai ivvaḷapeṭaikkum varuvittukkoḷka. māttirai niṟaittaṟkaṉṟi aṟikuṟi māttiraiyāy varumeṉṟu kōṭaṟku vēṟē yeṉṟār.

(u-m.)
"ilaṅṅku veṇpiṟaicū ṭīcaṉaṭi yārkkuk
kalaṅṅku neñcamilai kāṇ"

eṉavum,
"eṅṅ kiṟaivaṉuḷa ṉeṉpāy maṉaṉēyāṉ
eṅṅ keṉattirivā riṉ"

eṉavum,
"kalaṅṅkoṇṭa kaviṉmārpar"
"maṭaṅṅ kalanta maṉaṉē kaḷattu
viṭaṅṅ kalantāṉai vēṇṭu"

eṉavum,

"aṅṅ kaṉinta varuḷiṭattārk kaṉpuceytu
naṅṅ kaḷaṅkaṟuppā nām"

eṉavum ṅakaram kuṟiliṇaikkīḻum kuṟiṟkīḻum moḻikkiṭaiyiṉum kaṭaiyiṉum muṟaiyē aḷapeḻuntatu.
"kaṇṇ karuviḷai kārmullai kūreyiṟu
poṉṉ poṟicuṇaṅku pōḻvā yilavampū
miṉṉ ṉuḻaimaruṅku mētaku cāyalāḷ
eṉṉ piṟamakaḷā māṟu"

eṉa ṇakāramum ṉakāramum kuṟiṟkīḻ moḻiyīṟṟiṉ aḷapeḻuntaṉa.
"eḵḵ kilaṅkiya kaiyarā yiṉṉuyir
veḵḵku vārkkillai vīṭu"

eṉa āytam kuṟiṟkīḻ moḻikkiṭaiyiṉ aḷapeḻuntatu; piṟavumaṉṉa.

āytam oṟṟōṭum orupuṭaiyottavarutaliṉ īṇṭut tanturaittār. nakaram vitiyīṟāy varutaliṉ ataṉai vilakkātu, āytam oru moḻikkaṇṇum toṭarmoḻikkaṇṇum vititta mutaleḻuttukkaḷ irumaruṅku niṉṟeḻuppa iru ciṟakeḻuppa eḻumuṭalatupōl iṭaiyeḻuntu olippataṉṟi oruvāṟṟāṉum īṟāyvarum taṉmaiyataṉṟākaliṉ, aḵkaṭiya mutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ tirinta āytam aḵkāṉ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṭṭōṉṟiya āytam pōlat toṭarmoḻikkaṇ iṭai nilaiyāyataṉṟi vitiyīṟaṉṟeṉṟu ataṉuṇmai tuṇintu ataṉai iṟutikkaṇ vilakki, oṟṟaḷapeṭai nāṟpattiraṇṭeṉṟu mēluraittavāṟu iṅṅaṉam varutal kāṇka.

((kuṟṟiyalikaram))

NA_ca_93

yakaram varakkuṟa ḷuttiri yikaramum
acaiccoṉ miyāvi ṉikaramuṅ kuṟiya.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟiyalikaram varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) yakaram varumoḻikku mutalākivara nilaimoḻikku īṟāki niṉṟa kuṟṟiyalukarantirinta ikaramum miyāveṉṉum acaiccolliṉkaṇ ikaramum kuṟṟiyalikaraṅkaḷām e-ṟu.

eṉavē iṅṅaṉam varumāyiṉ ivveḻuttiṉṟoṭarpāṟ kuṟukumeṉpatu peṟṟām.

(u-m.) nākiyātu eḵkiyātu varakiyātu kokkiyātu kuraṅkiyātu alkiyātu eṉavum, kēṇmiyā ceṉmiyā eṉavum varum. piṟavu maṉṉa.

potuppaṭak kūṟiya kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟālum acaiccol miyāviṉālum kuṟṟiyalikaram muppattēḻāy varumāṟu kāṇka.

((kuṟṟiyalukaram))

NA_ca_94

neṭilo ṭāyta muyirvali meliyiṭai
toṭarmoḻi yiṟuti vaṉmaiyū rukaram
aḵkum piṟamēṟ ṟoṭaravum peṟumē.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟiyalukaram varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) taṉineṭiluṭaṉē āytamum uyirum valloṟṟum melloṟṟum iṭaiyoṟṟumākiya ellā veḻuttiṉuḷḷum yātāṉumoṉṟu toṭarappaṭṭa moḻiyiṟutikkaṇ valloṟṟukkaḷai ūrntuvanta ukaram taṉ māttiraiyiṟ kuṟukum; atu taṉ māttiraiyiṟ kuṟukutaṟkum moḻi niramputaṟkuṅ kāraṇam ivvōreḻuttuttoṭar taṉ māttiraiyiṉ amaiyātu piṟaveḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟum palavum mēlē toṭaravum peṟum e-ṟu.

neṭilai oṭukkoṭuttup pirittār, taṉineṭilātaliṉ. uyireṉpatu kuṟṟuyir neṭṭuyir iraṇṭiṉaiyum. toṭareṉṉum viṉaittokai vaṉmaiyūrukara meṉṉum ceyappaṭu poruḷoṭu muṭintatu. piṟaveṉṟatu pakāppatamēḻum pakupatamoṉpatu meṉṟu varaiyaṟuttavaṟṟiṉ iṅṅaṉam īṟṟayalum īṟumākak kūṟiya iraṇṭumoḻittu oḻinta aintum ēḻumāya eḻuttukkaḷai. toṭaravumeṉṟavummai iṟantatutaḻīi niṉṟatu. peṟumē yeṉṟatu, taṉineṭiloḻinta aintoṭarum vaṉmaiyūrukaram kuṟukutaṟkum moḻi niramputaṟkum mēṟṟoṭartalum iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyiṉ.

(u-m.) nāku, eḵku, varaku, palācu, kokku, kuraṅku, alku eṉa varum. piṟavu maṉṉa.

āciriyar tolkāppiyar.
"neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟum
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē" (moḻi. cū. 3.)

eṉat tokuttuk kūṟiyavāṟē kūṟātu ellā eḻuttaiyum kūṟuvār. avaṟṟaiyē āytamutal aintāka vakuttut taṉi neṭilōṭu āṟākak kūṟutal kuṟṟukarattai aṟuvakaiyāka vaḻaṅkutaṟku ilakkaṇan tōṉṟaveṉak koḷka. itu muṉ moḻintu kōṭaleṉṉum utti. avvāciriyar nuntaiyeṉṉu muṟaippeyarkkaṇ ukarattaik kuṟukkameṉṟār. aḵtu ivarkku uṭaṉpāṭaṉmaiyiṟ kūṟātoḻintār. itu maṟuttaleṉṉum matam; ēṉaiya uṭaṉpaṭal.

mayakkavitiyiṉmaiyiṉ; iṭaiyiṉantoṭarāta ṭakāra ṟakāraṅkaḷai ūrnta ukaramiṇṭiṉaiyum vilakkātu āṉaimukak kaṭavuḷai mummatatta ṉeṉṟāṟpōla ilakkaṇai yeṉṉum vitiyāṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram muppattāṟeṉṟu mēṟkūṟiyavāṟē vaṉmaiyūrukaram āṟōṭu neṭil mutaliya āṟaiyumuṟaḻa muppattāṟāy varumāṟu kāṇka.

((aikārakkuṟukkamum aukārakkuṟukkamum))

NA_ca_95

taṟcuṭ ṭaḷavoḻi yaimmū vaḻiyum
naiyu mauvu mutalaṟ ṟākum.

e-ṉ, aikāra aukārakkuṟukkaṅkaḷ varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) aikāram taṉṉaik karutit taṉpeyar kūṟumaḷaviṟ kuṟukātu, oḻinta moḻimutal kaṭai iṭaiyeṉṉu mūṉṟiṭattum muṉṉum piṉṉum irumaruṅkumāya eḻuttiṉ toṭarpāl taṉ māttiraiyiṟ kuṟukum. aukāramum taṉpeyar kūṟumaḷaviṟ kuṟukātu, oḻinta moḻi mutaṟkaṭ piṉvarumeḻuttiṉ toṭarpāl taṉ māttiraiyiṟ kuṟukum e-ṟu.

cuṭṭaḷaveṉpatu viṉaittokai. aḷaveṉṟār, eḻuttiṉ cāriyai toṭariṉum kuṟukumeṉpatu karuti. eṉavē ōreḻuttoru moḻiyāy nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyāyt toṭarntu taṉittu ōrōviṭattuvariṉ, taṉmāttirai kuṟukāteṉpatūum peṟṟām.

(u-m.) aippaci maippuṟam eṉavum, valaiyaṉ eṉavum, kuvaḷai eṉavum, mauval eṉavum varum.

iṉi aḷapeṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu aḷapeṉpataṉai aḷapeṭaiyākkit taṉṉaiccollutaṟkaṇṇum aḷapeṭuttaṟkaṇṇum kuṟukāteṉap poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar. taṉṉiyalpāya iraṇṭumāttiraiyiṉiṉṟum kuṟukutal illaṉavaṟṟai yoḻippār, vikārattāl mūṉṟumāttiraiyum nāṉkumāttiraiyumāy mikkolikkum aḷapeṭaiyai oḻikkavēṇṭāmaiyiṉ atu poruntāteṉka.

iṭavakaiyāṉ aikārakkuṟukkaṅkaḷ mūṉṟum, aukārak kuṟukkam oṉṟum varumāṟu kāṇka.

((makarakkuṟukkam))

NA_ca_96

ṇaṉamuṉṉum vaḵkāṉ micaiyumak kuṟukum.

e-ṉ, makarakkuṟukkam varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷil oṉṟu niṉṟutoṭarntiṭa ataṉmuṉvariṉum, vakaram vantu toṭarntiṭa ataṉmēl niṟpiṉum makaram taṉ araimāttiraiyiṟ kuṟukum e-ṟu.

(u-m.)
"pacuppōlvār muṟpaṭṭāṟ pāṟpaṭṭa cāṉṟōr
mucuppōla muḷkān tiruppar - pacuttāṉ
veruḷiṉu mellām veruḷumaḵ taṉṟi
maruḷiṉu mellā maruṇam" eṉavum,
"ticaiyaṟi mīkāṉum pōṉm" (pari. 10 : 55) eṉavum,

"tarum vaḷavaṉ" eṉavum varum.

iṉi ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷiṉ muṉvanta makaram ceyyumeṉṉum vāypāṭṭu muṟṟīṟṟateṉpatūum, avvīṟṟayalukaraṅkeṭa avvukaramūrnta ḷakara lakaraṅkaḷ īṟṟumakarattoṭu mayaṅkāmaiyiṉ mayaṅkutaṟkuriya ṇakara ṉakaramāyt tirintaṉa īṇṭu niṉṟa ṇakara ṉakarameṉpatūum, ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟuṭaṉ īroṟṟāy mayaṅkiṉ makaraṅ kuṟukumeṉpatūum, ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ kuṟukutal ceyyuṭkaṇṇateṉpatūum, kāṇum kūṉumeṉa iyalpākaniṉṟa ṇakara ṉakaraṅkaḷmuṉ ivvāṟu vantu makaram kuṟukāteṉpatūum, piṟavum mēlvarum cūttiraṅkaḷellāvaṟṟaiyumvēṇṭip paruntiṉvīḻvāy niṉṟa iccūttirattu uyttuṇarntu koḷka. iḵtu uyttuṇaravaippeṉṉumutti.

((āytakkuṟukkam))

NA_ca_97

laḷavīṟ ṟiyaipiṉā māyta maḵkum.

e-ṉ, āytakkuṟukkam varumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) lakāra ḷakāravīṟṟup puṇarcciyiṉāl varum. "kuṟilvaḻi laḷattav vaṇaiyi ṉāyta, mākavum peṟūum" (cū. 228) eṉṟa āytam irumaruṅkumāya eḻuttiṉ toṭarpāl taṉ araimāttiraiyiṟ kuṟukum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kaḵṟītu, muḵṭītu eṉa varum.

itu muṭiviṭaṅkūṟaleṉṉu mutti. ivvāytam kuṟukāteṉak kāṇṭalvirōtaṅ kūṟuvārumuḷar.

5. eḻuttiṉ uruvam

NA_ca_98

tollai vaṭiviṉa vellā veḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytu mekara mokaramey puḷḷi.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē uruvamāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ellāveḻuttum palavēṟuvakaippaṭṭa varaintu vaḻaṅkum paḻaiya vaṭiviṉai yuṭaiyavām; avvaṭiviṉavāy vaḻaṅkumiṭattut taṉittum uṭampūrntum varum ekaramum okaramum taṉimeykaḷum iyalpāya puḷḷiyaip piṟkālattu oḻittuvaraintu ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷōṭum uyirmeykaḷōṭum aiyappaṭa vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkiṉai yuṭaiyavaṉṟit tuṇiyappaṭum tollaivaṭiviṉatu uṟuppāya puḷḷiyaip peṟum e-ṟu.

(va-ṟu.) e e; o o; ke ke; ko ko; ka ka eṉa varum.

6. eḻuttiṉ māttirai

NA_ca_99

mūṉṟuyi raḷapiraṇ ṭāneṭi loṉṟē
kuṟilō ṭaiyauk kuṟukka moṟṟaḷa
paraiyoṟ ṟiuk kuṟukka māytam
kālkuṟaṇ maḵkā ṉāyta māttirai.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē māttiraiyāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) uyiraḷapeṭaikku māttirai mūṉṟām; neṭilukku māttirai iraṇṭām; kuṟilukkum aikārakkuṟukkattiṟkum aukārakkuṟukkattiṟkum oṟṟaḷapeṭaikkum taṉittaṉi māttirai oṉṟām; oṟṟiṟkum kuṟṟiyalikarattiṟkum kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟkum āytattiṟkum taṉittaṉi māttirai araiyām; makarakkuṟukkattiṟkum āytak kuṟukkattiṟkum taṉittaṉi māttirai kālām e-ṟu.

uyirmeykku aḷavukūṟātoḻintār, mēl, uyiraḷavāyeṉṟuraittaliṉ; iḵtu uraittāmeṉṉu mutti. uyiraḷapeṭai nāṉkumāttiraiyavātal āriṭattuḷḷum avai pōlpavaṟṟuḷḷum arukivantu ceyyuḷ vaḻuvamaitiyāy muṭitaliṉ, ataṉaiyoḻittu ellārkkum oppamuṭintu payiṉṟu varuvatu mūṉṟu māttiraiyēyākalāṉ, mūṉṟuyiraḷapeṉṟār.

NA_ca_100

iyalpeḻu mānta rimainoṭi māttirai.

e-ṉ, eḻuttoliyeḻucci, palavaṟṟaiyum aḷantu kōṭaṟkukkūṟiya orumāttiraiyeṉṉum kālacaṅkaiyiṉai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) māntaruṭaiya iyalpākaveḻum imaippoḻutum noṭippoḻutum orumāttirai yeṉṉum varaiyaṟaippoḻutām e-ṟu.

imaiyeṉṟatu imaittalai; noṭiyeṉṟatu olittalai. ivaiyiraṇṭum ākupeyarāyk kālattaiyuṇarttiniṉṟaṉa. iyalpeḻumeṉṉum peyareccam imai noṭiyeṉṉum peyarkaḷōṭu muṭintatu. eḻuttoli mutaliyavaṟṟai iyalpu keṭuttu oruvaṉ vēṇṭiyavāṟē yeḻuppiṉum avvāṟeḻāniṟkum, imaiyum noṭiyum iyalpukeṭutteḻuppa vēṇṭiṉum avvāṟē eḻātu iyalpākavē eḻāniṟkumātaliṉ iyalpeḻumeṉavum mēlaiccūttirattu eḻuttolikaḷai vēṇṭiyavāṟē eḻuppātu ivvaḷavāṉ aḷanteḻuppukveṉavum kūṟiṉār.

piṉṉatu niṟuttaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ iv aḷavukaruvi piṟkūṟappaṭṭatu.

NA_ca_101

āviyu moṟṟu maḷaviṟan ticaittalum
mēvu micaiviḷi paṇṭamāṟ ṟātiyiṉ.

e-ṉ, tavaḷaippāyttāy oṉṟiṭaiyiṭṭu niṉṟa mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) mutal cārpaṉaittumāya āviyum oṟṟum mēṟkūṟiya aḷaviṟantu mikkolittalaiyum poruntum, icaiviḷi paṇṭamāṟṟu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉkaṇ e-ṟu.

ātiyeṉṟamaiyāṉ, nāval kuṟippicai muṟaiyīṭu pulampaluṅ koḷka. icaiyiṉ aḷaviṟanticaikkuṅkāl āvi paṉṉiraṇṭumāttirai īṟākavum oṟṟup patiṉorumāttiraiyīṟākavum icaikku meṉṟār icainūlār; itu piṟanūṉ muṭintatu tāṉuṭaṉ paṭutaleṉṉum utti. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṉ aḷaviṟanticaikkuṅkāl ulakanaṭai piṟaḻāticaippatē varaiyaṟaiyeṉka.

nāvaleṉpatu neṟpōr teḻikkum pakaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷait turappatoru col;
"kāva luḻavar kaṭuṅkaḷattup pōrēṟi
nāvalōooo veṉṟicaikku nāḷōtai - māvalavaṉ
kolyāṉai mēliruntu kūṟṟicaittāṟ pōlumē
nalyāṉaik kōkkiḷḷi nāṭu" (muttoḷḷāyiram)

eṉpataṉālaṟika.
"kaḵḵḵ ṟeṉṉuṅ kallata rattam",
"cuḵḵḵ ṟeṉṉun taṇṭōṭṭup peṇṇai"

eṉpaṉa kuṟippicai: ēṉaiya poruḷtōṉṟikkiṭattaliṉ aḷaviṟanticaittal vantavaḻik kāṇka.

7. mutaṉilai

NA_ca_102

paṉṉī ruyiruṅ kacatana pamavaya
ñaṅavī raintuyir meyyu moḻimutal.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē moḻikkumutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) paṉṉiraṇṭuyirum uyirūrnta ippattu meyyum moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) aṭai, āṭai, iṭai, īṭu, uṭai, ūṭal, eṭu, ēṭu, aiyam, oti, ōti, auviyam eṉavum, kaḷi, cavaṭi, taḷir, nalam, paṭai, malai, vaḷam yavaṉar, ñamali, aṅṅaṉam eṉavum varum; piṟavu maṉṉa.

uyirpōl taṉittu mutalāntaṉmaiya allavātaliṉ uyir meyyeṉṟār. iḵtu iṉṉatalla tituveṉamoḻita leṉṉu mutti.

NA_ca_103

uū oō alavoṭu vammutal.

e-ṉ, potuvitiyuṭ ciṟappuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) innāṉkumallāta eṭṭuyirōṭu vakaram moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) vaḷi, vāḷi, viḷi, vīḻi, veḷi, vēḷai, vaikal, vauvu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_104

aā uū ōau yammutal.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) iv vāṟuyirōṭum yakaram moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) yavaṉar, yāṉai, yukam, yūki, yōkam, yauvaṉam eṉa varum.

mēlai oṭuvai varuvittuk koḷka.

NA_ca_105

aā eovvo ṭāku ñammutal.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) innāluyirōṭum ñakaram moḻikku mutalākum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) ñamali, ñālam, ñekiḻi, ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_106

cuṭṭiyā ekara viṉāvaḻi avvai
oṭṭi ṅacvu mutalā kummē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟucuṭṭum yāviṉāvum ekaraviṉāvumāya iṭaiccoṟkaḷiṉpiṉ akarattaiyoṭṭi ṅavvum moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) aṅṅaṉam, iṅṅaṉam, uṅṅaṉam, yāṅṅaṉam, eṅṅaṉam eṉa varum.

ivaṟṟai aṅku, eṅku eṉṟāṟpōla orumoḻikaḷeṉṟāl eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, aññāṉṟu eṉpaṉapōlap piḷavupaṭṭu iṭaiyē melloṟṟu mikkuvarutaliṉ toṭarmoḻikaḷēyāmeṉka; ṅaṉameṉpatu iṭattiṉaiyum taṉmaiyiṉaiyumuṇarttum palaporuḷorucollāy variṉum taṉittu varuntaṉmaiyataṉṟi muṭavaṉ kōlūṉṟivantāṟpōlac cuṭṭu viṉāvākiya iṭaiccoṟkaḷai muṉṉiṭṭu varutalāṉ vaḻiyeṉṟum, ēṉaiya meykaḷpōla mutalākāmaiyiṉ avvōṭeṉṉātu oṭṭiyeṉṟum, oruvāṟṟāṉ mutalātaliṉ iḻivu ciṟappāka ṅavvu meṉṟum kūṟiṉār. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟalāṉ ṅakaram moḻikku mutalākāteṉpārkku uṭaṉpaṭalum maṟuttalumāyp piṟartam mata mēṟkoṇṭu kaḷaivēyeṉṉum matampaṭak kūṟiṉāreṉṟuṇarka.

iṉi mutalāmeṉṟa pattuyirmeyyuḷ vakaramutaliya nāṉkiṉaiyum vitantukūṟavē oḻinta āṟu meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭuyirōṭum mutalā meṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ peṟṟām.

8. īṟṟunilai

NA_ca_107

āvi ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷamey
cāyu mukara nālāṟu mīṟē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē moḻikkīṟāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) taṉittum oṟṟōṭum varum āvimutalāka iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya irupattunāṉkeḻuttum moḻikku īṟām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) ā, ī, ū, ē, ai, o, au eṉavum; cuṭṭum, viṉāvum uvamavurupaṭiyumāya ōreḻuttorumoḻiyām iṭaiccollākiya a, i, u, e, o eṉavum; viḷa, palā, kari, kurī, kaṭu, marū, cē, tai, kō eṉavum; uriñ maṇ, porun maram, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vāḷ, aḵku eṉavum varum. kāṭṭātoḻintaṉa varuñ cūttirattāṟ kāṇka. kuṟṟukaramum uyirāy aṭaṅkumēṉum puṇarcci vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟa vēṟeṭuttōtiṉāreṉka.

NA_ca_108

kuṟṟuyi raḷapi ṉīṟām ekaram
meyyo ṭēlā tonnav voṭāmau
kakara vakaramo ṭāku meṉpa.

e-ṉ, kuṟṟuyirkku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuvitiyum ekarattiṟku eytiyatu orumaruṅkuvilakkalum okarattiṟkum aukārattiṟkum eytiyavaṟṟuṭ ciṟappuvitiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟuyir aḷapeṭaiyiṉkaṇ varivaṭiviṉ aṟikuṟiyāyt taṉittu īṟām; ekaram meyyō ṭīṟākātu; okaram nakaramoṉṟuṭaṉ īṟām; aukāram kakara vakaraṅkaḷiraṇṭuṭaṉum īṟā meṉṟu colluvar pulavar. e-ṟu.

(u-m.)
"āa vaḷiya valavaṉ" eṉavum, no eṉavum, kau vau eṉavum varum.

neṭil aḷapeṭuppuḻi varivaṭivil aṟikuṟiyāyvaruṅ kuṟṟuyir neṭiloṭu toṭarntu uṭampaṭumeyyuṭaṉ īṟāntaṉmaiyataṉṟit taṉittu niṟṟaliṟ kuṟṟuyiraḷapi ṉīṟāmeṉṟār. tāṉē īṟātalaṉṟik kuṟṟuyir aḷapiṉumīṟāmeṉa ummai koṭā toḻintār, uyirkaḻinta vuṭampai, "paṭumakaṉ kiṭaikkai kāṇūu" (puṟanā. 271) eṉṟār pōla, aṟikuṟi māttiraiyāy niṉṟataṉai iṅṅaṉam kuṟṟuyireṉṟataṉṟi aḵtu ōreḻut taṉṟākaliṉ, ekara okara aukāraṅkaḷai vitantōtavē ēṉaiyuyirkaḷ tamakkēṟṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāmeṉpatupeṟṟām.

mutalum cārpumāy vakuttu virittukkoṇṭē ellāveḻuttum uyirum meyyum āytamumāyaṭaṅkutaliṉ, mutalum īṟumākāta āytattai yoḻittu oḻinta uyirum meyyumāya iraṇṭiṉaiyum uyirmutal, meymmutal, uyirīṟu, meyyīṟeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭār. mēlvarum puṇarcciviti aṭaṅkutaṟporuṭṭeṉak koḷka.

NA_ca_109

niṉṟa neṟiyē yuyirmey mutalīṟē.

e-ṉ, eḻuttiṉatu mutalum īṟumāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) uyirmeyyiṉatu mutal yātu, ataṉ īṟu yāteṉiṉ, oṟṟumuṉṉum uyir piṉṉumāy olittu niṉṟa neṟiyē mey ataṉ mutalām, uyir ataṉ īṟām e-ṟu.

eṉavē uyirum oṟṟum āyta mutaliya oṉpatu cārpeḻuttukkaḷum ōreḻuttātaliṉ. avaṟṟiṟku mutalumīṟum avaiyēyāmeṉpatu aruttāpattiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. iḵtu eñciya colli ṉeytakkūṟa leṉṉu mutti. it toṭakkatta vallaṉa colliṉ muṭiviṉapporuḷ muṭitta leṉṉu mutti. muṉṉar moḻikku mutalīṟeṉṟavaṟṟuḷḷum piṉṉar iṭainilai pōlipatam puṇarpeṉpavaṟṟuḷḷum uyirmeyyaip pirittut taṉi meyyum taṉiyuyirumpōla vaittu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟaliṉ, moḻikku mutalum īṟuṅ kūṟutalaṉṟi eḻuttiṟku mutalumīṟum kūṟiṉāreṉṟuṇarka. ic cūttiram ciṅka nōkkam: aṟṟaṉṟi āṟṟoḻukkāy muṉṉiṉṟa moḻimutalīṟuraikkum cūttiraṅkaḷaiyē toṭarntuvanta teṉpārumuḷar; piṉ, "ṅammuṉkavvām" (cū. 111 eṉṟum, "iyalpiṉum vitiyiṉu niṉṟa vuyirmuṉ, kacatapa mikum" (cū. 165) eṉṟum uyirmeyyiṉ uyiraiyum meyyaiyum vēṟupirittōtuvatu ilakkaṇamaṉṟāy muṭiyumātaliṉ, atu poruntāteṉka.

eṇṇatikārattuḷ uyirmeyyai ōreḻuttāka eṇṇiṉār īṇṭu iraṇṭākavaittu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuva teṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, appoṭu peyta uppē pōla uyiroṭu puṇarttiya mey taṉṉaḷavu tōṉṟātu oṉṟāyniṟṟaliṉ oṉṟumāy, oṟṟu muṉṉum uyir piṉṉumāy olittu niṉṟa neṟiyāṉ iraṇṭumāy niṟṟaliṉeṉṟuṇarka. ivvāṟṟāṉ uyirmeyyeṉṉuñ col māttiraivakaiyāṉ ummait tokaippuṟattup piṟanta aṉmoḻit tokaiyākavum, olivakaiyāṉ ummait tokaiyākavum koḷḷappaṭum.

9. iṭainilai meymayakkam

NA_ca_110

kacatapa voḻitta ḷirēḻaṉ kūṭṭam
meymmayak kuṭaṉilai raḻavoḻit tīreṭ
ṭākumiv virupāṉ mayakku moḻiyiṭai
mēvu muyirmey mayakkaḷa viṉṟē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē moḻikku iṭainilaiyāmāṟu uṇarttuṅkāl uyiruṭaṉ uyirmayaṅkalum meyyuṭaṉ meymayaṅkalum ivviraṇṭum māṟi mayaṅkalumeṉa mūṉṟāka vakukkappaṭum: ivaṟṟuḷ uyiruṭaṉuyirkku mayaṅkum taṉmai iṉmaiyiṉ ataṉaiyoḻittu ēṉai yiraṇṭiṉaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) meypatiṉeṭṭaṉuḷ, kacatapa veṉṉu nāṉkaiyumoḻitta patiṉāṉku meyyum piṟameykaḷōṭu kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam vēṟṟunilai meymayakkamām; rakāra ḻakāra meṉṉu miraṇṭiṉaiyu moḻittu oḻinta patiṉāṟu meyyum tammoṭu kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam uṭaṉilai meymayakkamām; ivviraṇṭu pakuti mayakkamum moḻikku iṭaiyē varum; ivvāṟu meyyuṭaṉ mey mayaṅkutalaṉṟi uyiruṭaṉ meyyum meyyuṭaṉ uyirum māṟiuyiru meyyum mayaṅku mayakkattiṟku varaiyaṟai yiṉṟu; vēṇṭiyavāṟē mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

kūṭṭameṉiṉum mayakkameṉiṉum okkum. uṭaṉilai yeṉṟataṉāṟ piṟameyyeṉpatu peṟṟām. moḻiyeṉṟatu orumoḻi toṭarmoḻi yeṉṉum iraṇṭiṉaiyum. uyirum meyyum mayaṅkutaṟku iṭam vitantukūṟātu potuppaṭak kūṟiṉār. moḻiyiṉum uyirmeyyiṉum ōreḻuttiṉum varutaliṉ.

iccūttirattāṉ meyyuṭaṉ mey mayaṅkuṅkāṟ kacatapa veṉṉum nāṉkum tammoṭu tāmē mayaṅkumeṉavum, raḻaveṉṉum iraṇṭum tammoṭu piṟavē mayaṅkumeṉavum oḻintapaṉṉiraṇṭu meyyum tammoṭu tāmum piṟavum mayaṅkum eṉavum peṟṟām.

uyirum meyyum mayaṅkutaṟku utāraṇam: al vil eṉavum, kā eṉavum moḻikkaṇ mayaṅkiṉa; ka eṉa eḻuttiṉkaṇ mayaṅkiṉa. meyyuṭaṉ mey mayaṅkutaṟku utāraṇam mēlvarum ciṟappuc cūttirattiṟ kāṇka.

/ivviṭainilaiyai orumoḻi toṭarmoḻi yiraṇṭiṉuṅ koḷḷātu orumoḻikkēyeṉak koḷvāru muḷar. evviṭattu variṉum iraṇṭeḻuttu iṇaṅkip poruntum poruttuvāyai iṭainilaiyeṉ ṟōtalāṉum, iṇaṅkātaṉavariṉ vikārappaṭutalāṉum atu nūṟkaruttaṉṟeṉka.

iṉi, uyirmeymayakkaḷaviṉṟē yeṉpataṟku uyirmey muṉ uyirmey mayaṅkutal varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟeṉap poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar; iṅṅaṉam uyirmeyyai iraṇṭeḻuttākap pirittu mayakkaviti kūṟuvār. ataṉaiyē oṉṟāka vaittu mayakkaviti kūṟārātalāṉum kūṟiṉum iṭainilaimayakkamuḻuvatum iccūttirattu aṭaṅkāmaiyāṉum atu poruntāteṉka.

((meymmayakkam))

NA_ca_111

ṅammuṉ kavvām vammuṉ yavvē.

e-ṉ, vēṟṟunilai meymmayakkattuḷ vitantu ciṟappuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ṅakaramuṉṉark kakaramum vakaramuṉṉar yakaramum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kaṅkaṉ, tevyātu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_112

ñanamuṉ ṟammiṉam yakaramo ṭākum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭumey muṉṉarum tamakkiṉamākiya cakara takaraṅkaḷum yakaramum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kañcaṉ, uriñyātu; kantaṉ, porunyātu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_113

ṭaṟamuṉ kacapa meyyuṭaṉ mayaṅkum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭu meymmuṉṉarum kacapa veṉṉum mūṉṟumeyyum iṇaṅki mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kaṭkam, kaṭci, nuṭpam; kaṟka, kaṟciṟār, kaṟpa eṉavarum.

NA_ca_114

ṇaṉamuṉ ṉiṉaṅkaca ñapamaya vavvarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭu meymmuṉṉarum tamakkiṉamākiya ṭaṟakkaḷum kacañapamayava eṉṉum ēḻu meyyum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) viṇṭu, veṇkalam: veṇcōṟu, veṇñamali, veṇpal, veṇmalar, maṇyātu, maṇvalitu eṉavum, puṉṟalai, puṉkaṇ, puṉcey, puṉñamali, puṉpayir, puṉmalar, poṉyātu, poṉvalitu eṉavum varum.

NA_ca_115

mammuṉ payava mayaṅku meṉpa.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.0 makara meymmuṉṉarp pakara yakara vakaraṅkaḷ mayaṅkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

(u-m.) kampaṉ kalamyātu kalam valitu eṉa varum.

NA_ca_116

yaraḻa muṉṉar moḻimutaṉ meyvarum.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) immūṉṟu meymmuṉṉarum moḻimutaṟku vitantu koṇṭa pattumeyyum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) vēy vēr vīḻ; kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, ñāṉṟatu, yātu, valitu eṉavum; vēyṅkuḻal, ārṅkōṭu pāḻṅkiṇaṟu eṉavum varum.

curuṅkac collaleṉṉum vaṉappuvakaiyāṉ moḻimutaṉ meyyeṉak kūṟaliṉ yakarattiṉmuṉ yakaramayaṅkumuṭaṉilai īṇṭuk koḷḷātoḻika.

NA_ca_117

laḷamuṉ kacapa vayavoṉ ṟummē.

e-ṉ, ituvumatu.

(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭu meymmuṉṉarum, kacapavaya eṉṉum aintu meyyum mayaṅkum e-ṟu.

(u-m.) vēl, vāḷ; kaṭitu, ciṟitu, peritu, valitu, yātu eṉa varum.

((uṭaṉilai mayakkam))

NA_ca_118

raḻaval laṉatammuṟ ṟāmuṭa ṉilaiyum.

e-ṉ, uṭaṉilaimeymmayakkattiṉ ciṟappuviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) rakāra ḻakāramallaṉavākiya vēṟṟunilai meymmayakkattuṭ kūṟiya paṉṉiraṇṭu meyyum uṭaṉilaikkē uriya kacatapa veṉṉu nāṉkum tammiṟṟām uṭaṉiṉṟu mayaṅkum e-ṟu. (u-m.) aṅṅaṉam, avvai, aññāṉam, annīr, aṭṭu, aṟṟam, aṉṉai, aṇṇam, ammai, ayyam, alli, aḷḷal eṉavum; akku, accu, attu, appu eṉavum varum.

NA_ca_119

yaraḻavoṟ ṟiṉmuṉ kacatapa ṅañanama
īroṟ ṟāmraḻat taṉikkuṟi laṇaiyā.

e-ṉ, taṉimeyyuṭaṉ taṉimeyyāyum mayaṅkuvaṉa iṉaiyaveṉpatūum, moḻikku uṟuppāka mayaṅkātaṉa iṉaiyaveṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) yaraḻa veṉṉu mūṉṟoṟṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ muṉ mayaṅkumeṉa vititta pattoṟṟuḷ iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya eṭṭoṟṟum taṉimeyyum uyirūrnta meyyumāy mayaṅkuvaṉavaṉṟi īroṟṟāyum mayaṅkuvaṉavām; rakāra ḻakāra voṟṟum taṉikkuṟilum mayaṅkun taṉmaiyavāyiṉum moḻikkuṟuppāka varuvatum niṟpatumāy mayaṅkā e-ṟu.

īroṟṟumāmeṉṉum ummaiyum moḻikku uṟuppākaveṉṉum coṟkaḷum eñci niṉṟaṉa.

(u-m.) vēykkuṟai, vērkkuṟai, vīḻkkuṟai; ciṟai, talai, puṟam eṉavum, vēyṅkuḻal, ārṅkōṭu, pāḻṅkiṇaṟu; kāyntaṉam, nērntaṉam, vāḻntaṉam eṉavum varum.

ēṉaiya vantavaḻik kāṇka.

aṇaiyā veṉṟa ākātaṉavaṟṟiṟku utāraṇaṅkāṭṭalākāmai yuṇarka.

NA_ca_120

laḷamey tirinta ṉaṇamuṉ makāram
naintī roṟṟāñ ceyyu ḷuḷḷē.

e-ṉ, ceyyuṭkuriyavāy īroṟṟuṭaṉiṟpaṉa iṉaiyaveṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) lakāra ḷakāra voṟṟuttirinta ṉakāra ṇakāra muṉvarum makara voṟṟu muṉṉarkkūṟiyavāṟē kuṟuki avaṟṟuṭaṉ īroṟṟuṭaṉilaiyām, ceyyuḷakattu e-ṟu.

(u-m.)
"ticaiyaṟi, mīkāṉum pōṉm" (pari. 10. 55) eṉavum
"mayiliyaṉ mātar maruṇm" eṉavum varum.

makāranainteṉa aṉuvatittār, makaraṅkuṟukutaṟkut toṭarum ṉakaraṇakaraṅkaḷ lakaraḷakaran tirintaṉaveṉpatūum akkuṟukkam ceyyuṭkaṇṇateṉpatūum uyttuṇartaṟkeṉka.

NA_ca_121

tampeyar moḻiyiṉ mutalu mayakkamum
immuṟai māṟiyu miyalu meṉpa.

e-ṉ, moḻimutal īṟu iṭainilaikaṭku āvatoru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) eḻuttukkaḷ tampeyarkaḷai moḻintu nilaimoḻi varumoḻiyākap puṇarkkumiṭattu, ellā moḻi mutaṟkum iṅṅaṉam vitittaṉavum vilakkiyaṉavumākiya ellā veḻuttum mutalām; mayakkattiṟku iṅṅaṉam vitittaṉavum vilakkiyaṉavumākiya ellā veḻuttum mayaṅkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

iṅṅaṉam ellāveḻuttum taṉittaṉi ataṟkatuvē mutalāka varumeṉavē, īṟṟiṟku vitittaṉavum vilakkiyaṉavumākiya ellāveḻuttum taṉittaṉi ataṟkatuvē īṟāka varumeṉac collātē aruttāpattiyāṉ amaitaliṉ īṟumeṉa mikaipaṭak kūṟātoḻintār.

(u-m.) "avaṟṟuḷ, laḷaḵkāṉ muṉṉar yavavun tōṉṟum" (tol. nūṉ. cū. 24) eṉpuḻi lakaram mutalākiyum kūṉiṟuti ḷakara voṟṟōṭu mayaṅkiyum niṉṟatu. cepperitu eṉpuḻi ekaram meyyō ṭīṟāy niṉṟatu. piṟavum varaiyaṟaiyiṉṟi varutal kāṇka.

āytattiṟkup piṟappatikārattuḷ varuvāy kūṟiyavaḻi mayakkaviti amaintu kiṭantamaiyāṉum taṉpeyar moḻiya vārāmaiyāṉum kūṟātoḻintār.

((10. pōli))

((moḻiyiṟutippōli))

NA_ca_122

makara viṟuti yaḵṟiṇaip peyariṉ
ṉakaramō ṭuṟaḻā naṭappaṉa vuḷavē.

e-ṉ, niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē pōliyāmāṟu uṇarttuvāṉ toṭaṅki moḻiyiṟutippōli uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pālpakā aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷiṉiṭattu iṟuti makaram ṉakarattōṭu ottu naṭappaṉa uḷavām e-ṟu.

makaraviṟuti eṉṟataṉāṟ pālpakāvaḵṟiṇaip peyareṉpatu peṟṟām. makaram ṉakarattōṭottalāvatu peyariṟutikkaṇ makaram niṉṟa nilaikkaḷattu ṉakaram niṟpiṉum vēṟṟumaiyiṉṟiyottal.

(u-m.)
"akaṉamarntu ceyyā ḷuṟaiyu mukaṉamarntu
nalvirun tōmpuvā ṉil" (kuṟaḷ, 84.)

itaṉuḷ, akam - akaṉ, mukam - mukaṉ eṉavum; talam - talaṉ, nilam - nilaṉ, kalam - kalaṉ, nalam - nalaṉ, valam - valaṉ eṉavum varutal kāṇka. piṟavumaṉṉa.

uṟaḻānaṭappaṉavuḷa veṉṉuñ collāṟṟalāṉ uṟaḻātaṉa perumpāla veṉka. avai: vaṭṭam. kuṭṭam, māṭam, kūṭam mutalāyiṉa. pālpakāvaḵṟiṇaippeyariṭattu iyalpāya makaraviṟutinilaiyiṉ ṉakaram pōliyāy varumeṉavē akaṉ mukaṉ mutaliya coṟkaḷaip pālpakutta āṭūuvaṟi colleṉak koḷḷaṟka eṉpatūum, ekiṉ, payiṉ, kuyiṉ, vayiṉ mutaliya ṉakaravīṟṟuppeyarkku ivvaiyappāṭillai yeṉpatūum peṟṟām.

iṉi, ivviṟutippōliyai muṟkūṟiyataṉāl, curumpu - curumpar, arumpu - arumpar, cāmpal-cāmpar, pantal - pantar mutaliyaṉavum koḷka.

(moḻi mutaṟpōliyum moḻiyiṭaippōliyum)

NA_ca_123

aai mutaliṭai yokkuñ cañayamuṉ.

e-ṉ, moḻimutaṟ pōliyum moḻiyiṭaip pōliyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) moḻimutaliṉum iṭaiyiṉum akara aikāraṅkaḷ vēṟṟumai yiṉṟi ottu naṭappaṉavām, cakara ñakara yakaraṅkaṭku muṉṉar varumāyiṉ e-ṟu.

īṇṭu muṉṉeṉṟatu, kālamuṉṉeṉak koḷka.

(u-m.) pacal - paical (paiyal), mañcu - maiñcu, mayal - maiyal eṉavum; amaccu - amaiccu, ilañci - ilaiñci, arayar - araiyar eṉavum varum. piṟavumaṉṉa.

((moḻiyiṭaippōli))

NA_ca_124

aikāṉ yavvaḻi navvoṭu cilvaḻi
ñaḵkā ṉuṟaḻu meṉmaru muḷarē.

e-ṉ, ituvum moḻiyiṭaippōli uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) moḻiyiṭaikkaṭ cilaviṭattu aikārattiṉ piṉṉum yakaravoṟṟiṉpiṉṉum iyalpāyvarum nakarattōṭu ñakaram uṟaḻu meṉṟu colvārumuḷar e-ṟu.

ippōli arukivarutaliṟ cilvaḻiyeṉṟum, ellārkkum oppa muṭintataṉṟākaliṉ eṉmarumeṉṟum kūṟiṉār.

(u-m.)
"ceyññiṉṟa nīla malarkiṉṟa tillaicciṟ ṟampalavaṉ
maiññiṉṟa voṇkaṇ malaimakaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻntuniṟka
neyaññiṉ ṟeriyum viḷakkotta nīla maṇimiṭaṟṟāṉ
kaiññiṉṟa vāṭalkaṇṭāṟ piṉṉaik kaṇkoṇṭu kāṇpateṉṉē"

(tē. kōyil, tirunā, tiruviruttam "pāḷaiyuṭai", 5) eṉavum, aiññūṟu eṉavum varum.

iccūttiram piṟaṉkōṭkūṟa leṉṉu mutti.
"maṇyātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē"
maṇñātta kōṭṭa maḻakaḷiṟu tōṉṟumē" eṉavum
"poṉyātta tārpuravi parikkumē"
"poṉñātta tārpuravi parikkumē"

eṉavum varumoḻi viṉaikkumuṉṉar yā niṉṟa nilaikkaḷattu ñā niṟpiṉu mokkumpōlikku āciriyar tolkāppiyar,
"ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
viṉaiyō raṉaiya veṉmaṉār pulavar" (tokai. cū. 4)

eṉac cūttirañ ceytavāṟu ivvāciriyarum cūttirañ ceyyā toḻital iṟantatuvilakkaleṉṉum utti. piṟavu maṉṉa.

immūṉṟu cūttirattāṉuṅkūṟiya pōlikaḷai iṭainilai mayakkattiṉ pāṟpaṭuttip poruḷ kūṟuvārumuḷar; iṅṅaṉam kūṟiya eḻuttukkaḷ moḻikkuṟuppāki oṉṟu niṉṟa nilaikkaḷattu maṟṟoṉṟu atupōla moḻinirappa niṟpataṉṟi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu mayaṅki iraṇṭeḻuttum uṭaṉiṟpatiṉmaiyāṉum, mutalīṟiṭainilaikaṭkup puṟaṉaṭaiyuṅkūṟik kuṟai vaṟuttamaiyāṉum, `uṟaḻānaṭappaṉa' eṉṟum `okkum' eṉṟum, `uṟaḻum' eṉṟum uvamavurupu koṭuttu immūṉṟu cūttiramum kūṟutalāṉum atu poruntāteṉka.

((eḻuttuppōli))

NA_ca_125

ammuṉ ikaram yakara meṉṟivai
eytiṉ aiyot ticaikkum avvō
ṭuvvum vavvum auvō raṉṉa.

e-ṉ, eḻuttuppōli uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) akarattiṉmuṉṉar ikaramum yakaravoṟṟum varumāyiṉ aikāram pōṉṟolikkum; akarattōṭu ukaramum vakaravoṟṟum varumāyiṉ aukāram pōṉṟolikkum e-ṟu.

uyirōṭuyirkku mayakkamiṉmaiyiṉ iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya kuṟṟuyirkaḷai valintu mayakkikkūṟiṉ. aikāra aukārampōl icaittalāṉum kuṟṟoṟṟukkaḷ oṉṟaraimāttiraiyavātaliṉ iraṇṭu māttiraiyavākiya aikāra aukāraṅkaḷōṭu ovvāmaiyiṉ oli vakaiyāṉ mayakkik kūṟiṉ avai otticaittalāṉum avaṟṟuḷḷum vakaram piṟappu vēṟupāṭṭāṉ oliyum orupuṭaiyottalāṉum uytiṉeṉṟār.

(u-m.) ai = ai ay = ai kai = kai, kay = kai au = au, av = au, kau = kau eṉavum,
"kayvēl kaḷiṟṟoṭu pōkki varupavaṉ
meyvēl paṟiyā nakum" (kuṟaḷ, 774) eṉavum,
"avviya neñcattā ṉākkamuñ cevviyāṉ
kēṭu niṉaikkap paṭum" (kuṟaḷ, 169) eṉavum varum.

icceyyuṭkaṇ toṭainōkki aikāra aukāram niṉṟa nilaikkaḷattap pōli vantavāṟu kāṇka.

((eḻuttiṉ cāriyai))

NA_ca_126

meykaḷ akaramu neṭṭuyir kāramum
aiauk kāṉu mirumaik kuṟiliv
viraṇṭoṭu karamumāñ cāriyai peṟumpiṟa.

e-ṉ, mēl eḻuttilakkaṇaṅkūṟiya mukattāṟ pōnta eḻuttiṉ cāriyaikaḷait tokuttu iṉamaṭaittal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) meykaḷ akaraccāriyaiyum, neṭṭuyirkaḷ kārac cāriyaiyum, avaṟṟuḷ aikāra aukāraṅkaḷ kāraccāriyaiyuṭaṉē kāṉcāriyaiyum, uyirkkuṟilum uyirmeykkuṟilum kāramkāṉeṉṉum ivviraṇṭuṭaṉē karaccāriyaiyum peṟum e-ṟu.

meykaḷai muṟkūṟiṉār, avaṟṟiṟku akaraccāriyai iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyiṉ. meykaḷ avvumeṉṉātu akaramu meṉṟār, aṅṅaṉam karam kāram kāṉkaḷ variṟ cāriyai akarattiṉatu cāriyaiyeṉṟukōṭaṟku, āñcāriyaiyeṉṟār, kāra mutaliya cāriyaikaḷai ēṟṟapeṟṟi kōṭaṟku.

(u-m.) ka ṅa eṉavum, ākāram aikāram aukāram eṉavum, aikāṉ aukāṉ eṉavum, akāram aḵkāṉ akaram makāram maḵkāṉ makaram eṉavum varum. piṟavu maṉṉa.

piṟa veṉṟamaiyāṟ kuṟiloṭu kāṉpuṇaruṅkāl iṭaiyē āytattōṟṟamum vaḻaṅkum. piṟacāriyaikaḷum koḷka. uyirmey neṭilum āytamum kūṟātoḻintār, uyirmey neṭil iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya mūṉṟucāriyaiyum ēlāmaiyāṉum āytam piṟavaḻi vārāmaiyiṉ moḻiyiṭaippaṭuttu ammoḻiyiṟutikkaṭ piṟacāriyaitantu uyirvarukkattu iṟutikkaṇṇē vaḻaṅkutalāṉumeṉka.

vikaṟpattiṉmuṭittaleṉṉum uttiyāṉ mēl virintu muṭintu kiṭantaṉavaṟṟaip pulappaṭavēṇṭi īṇṭuttokuttu muṭittalāṉ iccūttiram muṭintatu muṭittaleṉṉum utti.

((puṟaṉaṭai))

NA_ca_127

moḻiyāyt toṭariṉu muṉṉaṉait teḻuttē.

e-ṉ, ivviyalukku āvatoru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) ivveḻuttukkaḷ patamāyiṉum appatam tammoṭum urupoṭum puṇariṉum avaṟṟāṟ piṟaḻātu muṉṉark kūṟiya appattilakkaṇattaiyum uṭaiyavām e-ṟu.

eḻuttu, cuṇṇattiṉkaṇ aricaṉa mutaliya pōlātu mālaiyiṉkaṇ malarpōla avaṟṟil niṟṟaliṉ muṉṉaṉait teṉṟār. eḻutteṉpatu cātiyorumaiyātaliṉ, avaṉaitteṉṉum orumaiyoṭu muṭintatu. muṉṉaṉaittātal pataviyal mutaliya nāṉkiyaluḷḷuṅ kāṇka.

((eḻuttiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.))

((iraṇṭāvatu pataviyal))

((patam))

NA_ca_128

eḻuttē taṉittun toṭarntum poruṭariṟ
patamā matupakāp patampaku patameṉa
irupā lāki yiyalu meṉpa.

e-ṉ, niṟutta muṟaiyāṉē patattiṉatu ilakkaṇam uṇarttuvāṉ toṭaṅki eḻuttāṟ patamātalum, aḵtu ittuṇaiyateṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) eḻuttukkaḷ tāmē ovvoṉṟu taṉittum iraṇṭu mutalākat toṭarntum piṟaporuḷait tarumāyiṉ atu patamām; appatam papakāppatameṉavum pakupatameṉavum iraṇṭu pakutiyatāki naṭakkumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

eḻuttāṉāya patam aricaṉa mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ camaittacuṇṇam pōlātu malarāṟ camaitta mālaipōl niṟṟaliṉ eḻuttē yeṉṟum, ōreḻuttēṉum piṟaporuḷ tarutaliṟ patamāmeṉpār taṉitteṉṟum, oṉṟu iraṇṭu palaveṉpaṉa vaṭanūl vaḻakku, oṉṟallaṉa vellām palaveṉpatu tamiḻ naṭaiyātaliṉ vaṭanūl vaḻakkuppaṟṟi āciriyar tolkāppiyar,
"ōreḻut torumoḻi yīreḻut torumoḻi
iraṇṭiṟan ticaikkun toṭarmoḻi yuḷappaṭa
mūṉṟē moḻinilai tōṉṟiya neṟiyē" (moḻi. cū. 12)

eṉṟārkku iraṇṭeḻuttut toṭarntaṉa vallaveṉiṉ atu moḻiyumaṉṟeṉpār taṉinilai yoḻintavaṟṟait toṭarnteṉṟum, iṟiñi miṟiñiyeṉiṟ ṟoṭarumēṉum taṉṉaiyuṇarttu maṉṟippiṟaporuḷ tārāmaiyiṟ patamākāteṉpār poruḷ tariṉeṉṟum.
"taṉṉai yuṇartti ṉeḻuttām piṟaporuḷaic
cuṭṭutaṟ kaṇṇēyāñ col"

eṉpavākaliṟ poruḷeṉṟatu piṟaporuḷeṉpār uṇarttiṉeṉṉātu tariṉeṉṟum, aṇuveṉṉum olinuṭpattāṉ eḻuttāṉāṟpōla (cū. 58) eḻutteṉṉum oli nuṭpattāṉ moḻiyāmeṉpār patamāmeṉṟum, ippataviyalukku mēṟkoḷ āriyamē yeṉpār moḻiyeṉṉātu patameṉṟum kūṟiṉār.

ic cūttirattuḷ toṭarntumeṉṟatu piṟarnūṟ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭaleṉṉum matam.

((ōreḻuttorumoḻi))

NA_ca_129

uyirmavi lāṟun tapanavi laintum
kavacavi ṉālum yavvi loṉṟum
āku neṭiṉotu vāṅkuṟi liraṇṭō
ṭōreḻut tiyalpata māṟēḻ ciṟappiṉa.

e-ṉ, mēl eḻuttut taṉittum toṭarntum orumoḻiyāmeṉṟār, avaṟṟuḷ ōreḻuttorumoḻi ivai eṉpatūum, ittuṇaiya veṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) uyir varukkattum mavvarukkattum avvāṟum, tavvarukkattum pavvarukkattum navvarukkattum aivaintum, kavvarukkattum vavvarukkattum cavvarukkattum nannāṉkum, yavvarukkattu oṉṟum ākam neṭṭeḻuttāṉākiya moḻi nāṟpatum: novvum tuvvumākum kuṟṟeḻuttāṉākiya moḻi iraṇṭuṭaṉē ōreḻuttāṉākiya moḻi innāṟpattiraṇṭum ciṟappiṉavām e-ṟu.

(u-m.) ā ī ū ē ai ō e-m, mā mī mū mē mai mō e-m. tā tī tū tē tai e-m, pā pū pē pai pō e-m, nā nī nē nō e-m, kā kū kai kō e-m, vā vī vai vau e-m, cā cī cē cō e-m, yā e-m, no tu e-m varum.

ivaṟṟuḷ ū - iṟaicci; ō - matakunīr tāṅkum palakai: pē - nurai; nē - aṉpu; cō - araṇ; no tu eṉṉum kuṟiliraṇṭum tuṉpi puci eṉṉum ēval.

ivai ciṟappiṉaveṉavē vakaravīṟṟuc cuṭṭup peyarp poruḷai oppumaiyāṉ uṇarttiniṟṟalāṉ au eṉṉum ōreḻuttoru moḻiyum cuṭṭu viṉā uvamaipporuḷait tarum iṭaiccollātalāṟ kuṟṟuyiraintāṉāya ōreḻuttoru moḻikaḷum kau veṉṉum uyirmeyyāṉāya ōreḻuttoru moḻiyum ivaipōlvaṉa piṟavum ciṟappillaṉaveṉak koḷka.

((toṭareḻuttorumoḻi))

NA_ca_130

pakāppata mēḻum pakupata moṉpatum
eḻuttī ṟākat toṭaru meṉpa.

e-ṉ, toṭareḻuttorumoḻiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pakāppatam iraṇṭeḻuttu mutal ēḻeḻuttīṟākavum, pakupatam iraṇṭeḻuttu mutal oṉpateḻuttīṟākavum toṭarumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.

(u-m.) aṇi, aṟam, akalam, aruppam, taruppaṇam, uttiraṭṭāti e-m: kūṉi, kūṉaṉ, kuḻaiyaṉ, poruppaṉ, ampalavaṉ, araṅkattāṉ, uttirāṭattāṉ, uttiraṭṭātiyāṉ e-m varum. eḻuntirukkiṉṟār, naṭattuvippikkiṟār, naṭattuvippikkiṟārkaḷ, naṭattuvippikkiṉṟārkaḷ, naṭattuvippikkiṉṟavarkaḷeṉap pakuti mutaliya uṟuppu vēṟupaṭṭu varuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku ivvaraiyaṟai illaiyeṉka.

((pakāppatam))

NA_ca_131

pakuppāṟ payaṉaṟ ṟiṭukuṟi yāki
muṉṉē yoṉṟāy muṭintiyal kiṉṟa
peyarviṉai yiṭaiyuri nāṉkum pakāppatam.

e-ṉ, mēṟpatam pakāppatam pakupatameṉa iraṇṭu pakutiyatāy naṭakkumeṉṟār, avaṟṟuṭ pakāppatam iṉaiyaveṉpatūum, ittuṇaiyaveṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.

(i-ḷ.) pakupatampōl itaṉaip pakuttup pārkkiṉ appakuppāṟ pakuti vikuti mutaliya uṟuppiṉ payaṉviḷaivatiṉṟik kāraṇattāṉ varum appatampōlātu iṭukuṟiyāyp pakuti vikuti mutaliya āṟuṟuppāṉum piṉpu muṭikkappaṭum appatam pōlātu paṭaippukkālantoṭṭu oṉṟāy muṭintu naṭakkiṉṟa peyarccol, viṉaiccol, iṭaiccol, uriccol eṉṉum nāṉkum pakāppatamām e-ṟu.

pakupatattiṟku maṟutalaippaṭṭateṉpār ivvāṟu kūṟiṉār.
